# MC Server Manager — Plan 9: UI tests and polish

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Close the roadmap: crash-loop detection with the agent's `Event` message and its view in the web, `StreamDropped`, node token rotation (`RotateToken`), the Settings page, an allowlist proxy for the Docker socket for mc-router, the items parked from plans 1–8, and a minimal Playwright suite against the deployment compose file (with screenshots of the screens pending visual review).

**Architecture:** The agent wraps its Docker engine to annotate the stops it requests (`crashloop.Intents`), counts the `die` events with exit code ≠ 0 that nobody requested and, with more than 3 in one hour, stops the server and **latches** it (a persisted mark in bbolt tied to the spec version) until a «Start» or a new spec; each incident goes out as an `Event` (Envelope 24, unsolicited) through its own queue. The panel stores the events in the `events` table, publishes them on the `events` WS topic and, with `crash_loop`, sets the desired state to stopped. Token rotation uses a **pending** hash in `nodes` that the panel accepts alongside the current one until the agent connects with it. Settings stores the public URL and port range in `settings` (with the environment as default value). mc-router stops receiving the Docker socket: the agent serves it a filtered socket (list, inspect without environment, events, and start/stop only for servers with non-reserved hostnames). The E2E brings up the deployment compose file with the `mcsm-test-e2e` prefix, a second node on `docker:dind` and headless Playwright in a container.

**Tech Stack:** Go 1.27.2 (agent: new `crashloop` and `dockerproxy`; `reconcile`, `app`, `store`, `conn`, `logs`, `router`, `docker`); protobuf with buf 1.73 (`pnpm gen`); Node 26, pnpm 12, Fastify 5, `node:sqlite`, zod 4; React 19, TanStack Query/Router, shadcn/ui (Radix), uPlot; Vitest 4 on node (no jsdom); Playwright `@playwright/test` 1.64.0 in the image `mcr.microsoft.com/playwright:v1.64.0-noble`; Docker 29 (`docker:29-dind` for the second node).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-mc-server-manager-design.md` (§4 `nodes`/`settings`, §5.2 `StreamDropped`, §5.3 `Event`, §8 Settings, Nodes → detail with metrics, Server → History (jobs and events), §9 token rotation and Docker socket, §10 crash loops, §11 Playwright). Scope and acceptance: `docs/superpowers/plans/README.md` (Plan 9). Coordination: `docs/superpowers/plans/COORDINATION.md` (Envelope 120–129 and 24 = `Event`; migrations `080_*`–`089_*`; anchors `plan-9`, which already exist in all shared files). Controller inputs: `/home/lobo/dev/mc-server-manager/.superpowers/sdd/plan-9-inputs.md`. Format model: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-12-08-mc-router.md`.

**Plan verification (dry run):** the code for Tasks 1–33 was applied as-is to copies of `master` (@97cfade) in a temporary directory: `buf lint` and `buf breaking --against master` green; agent with `gofmt`, `go vet` (also `-tags integration`) and `go test -race ./...` green, and the router integration test (Task 13) with a real mc-router 1.47.1 **through the proxy** green in 89 s (discovery by hostname, autoscale with a list, sleep and wake up; the only denied request was the `create` from the test itself); panel: `tsc` and Vitest green (shared 290, api 527, web 316) and `vite build` correct; the E2E package compiles and the compose override merges as expected. The E2E with real Docker (Task 34) was not run in the dry run. If something does not compile when applied, the most likely cause is that the branch has changed since then: adapt the name, not the design.

## Global Constraints

- Branch `feat/plan-9` in `.worktrees/plan-9`, created from `master` @ `97cfade` (Plans 1–8 integrated). The parallel tracks go in their own worktrees (see «Batches»). All commands start with `cd <worktree> && …`; `eval "$(mise env)"` before `go`/`buf`. Only write to your own worktree.
- All the «Lessons learned» in `CLAUDE.md` are constraints. In particular: `rec.Apply`/`rec.Remove`, observer without re-entering, `App.life` for what mutates Docker or disk, `startOrDiscard` for every `Start` of a server, `toServerSpecMessage(row, curseforgeApiKey)` in every spec emitter, `serversService.patch` to change the spec, `HttpError` + global handler, `apiPath` in every route with values, `mergeIntoCached`, `useTabGuard` + `useBlocker` in forms with dirty state, `dialogSubmit` in dialogs with `<form>`, no `crypto.randomUUID()`, tests without `time.Sleep`/`setTimeout` to synchronise (except polling external resources in `integration` tests and in the E2E).
- Protocol: additions only. `Envelope.body` 24 `Event`, 120 `StreamDropped`, 121 `RotateToken`; the new messages go at the end of `agent.proto` under the anchor `// ── plan-9 ──`. `buf breaking --against ".git#ref=master"` green; after touching the proto, `pnpm gen` and the generated output is committed. No new `Error.code` values (`invalid_request`, `io_error`, `unsupported`).
- SQL migrations `080_events` and `081_nodes_pending_token` under the anchor `// ── plan-9 ──` of the `migrations` array. An applied migration is never edited.
- Server hash: **nothing in this plan changes the servers' `BuildContainer`**; `TestHashGolden` stays green. The router hash does change (Task 13: mounts the proxy socket): the router is recreated once when the agent is updated, and this is documented.
- Docker on this machine: there are **production** containers of the user (`mcsm-panel-1`, `mcsm-agent-1`, `mcsm-agent`, network `mcsm`, volumes `mcsm_*`, images `mcsm-*:latest`, ports 8080 and 25565–25600). No task touches them: every test resource carries the prefix `mcsm-test-` (containers, networks, volumes, images; servers with slug `test-e2e-…` → container `mcsm-test-e2e-…`), uses ports 18080 and 26600–26620, and is removed at the end. `docker system prune`, mass deletions or `down` of another project are forbidden. The data directories for dind tests go in `deploy/data/` (never under `/tmp`: dind mounts its tmpfs there).
- UI and error messages in Spanish; identifiers in English. Conventional commits in Spanish, small, ending with `Co-Authored-By: Claude <Model> 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>` with the model of the agent implementing the task (Haiku for mechanical-dev, Sonnet for go-agent-dev/panel-api-dev/web-ui-dev/infra-dev, Opus for systems-expert). Never `git push`, `--no-verify` or rewriting history; `git add` with concrete paths.
- Verification per area before each commit: agent `gofmt -w` on the touched Go files and `(cd agent && gofmt -l . && go vet ./... && go test -race ./...)`; panel `pnpm --filter <package> test` and `pnpm -r typecheck`; web also `pnpm --filter @mcsm/web build`.

## Review Focus

1. **Crash loop miscounted or wrongly stopped.** Own stops (Stop/Kill/Remove from the agent, mc-router stop due to inactivity, backup or restore) counted as a crash; a `restartPolicy` that restarts the container between the notice and the stop; a disconnected panel that, when it comes back, forwards «running» in `SyncSpecs` and starts it again. Expected: only the `die` events with exit code ≠ 0 that nobody requested count; with more than 3 in one hour the agent stops and **latches** the server (it survives `SyncSpecs` with the same version and agent restarts) until «Start» or a new version; the panel sets the desired state to stopped. Tests: Task 3 (`Intents`, `Tracker`), Task 4 (latch versus `StoreForce`/`Power`/new version), Task 5 (`onDie` with exit 0, requested stop and reserved server), Task 15 (`crash_loop` → desired stopped).
2. **Token rotation that leaves a node orphaned.** Lost Ack, reconnection with the new token before the panel accepts it, two rotations in a row, old agent. Expected: the agent saves the token **before** the Ack and reconnects afterwards; the panel accepts the pending token alongside the current one until the first connection with the new one (then the old one stops working); a second rotation while a recent pending one exists is 409; `unsupported` leaves everything as it was. Tests: Task 7 (order save → Ack → reconnect, token in the dial), Task 17 (pending accepted, promotion, old rejected, 409, `unsupported`).
3. **Docker socket proxy too open or too closed.** Routes that slip through (percent-encoding, `..`, `//`, `containers/create`, `exec`), the environment with secrets in an inspect, starting a reserved or latched server; or, the other way round, mc-router not discovering servers. Tests: Task 12 (`Classify` table, `RawPath`, `StripEnv`, 403/409), Task 13 (integration with real mc-router: discovery, autoscale and `create` denied through the proxy).
4. **Settings that leave the panel unusable.** Malformed public URL (WS, enrollment command), empty or reversed port range, password change that leaves old sessions open, initial setup token that blocks enrollment. Tests: Task 18, Task 19, Task 34 (the E2E uses the setup token and the port range from Settings).
5. **Security headers that break the web** (CSP with the inline styles of Radix/uPlot, the WebSocket, CodeMirror). Tests: Task 20 (headers), Task 34 (no CSP errors in the browser console on all pages visited).
6. **The E2E touches production** (containers, network `mcsm`, volumes `mcsm_*`, ports 8080/25565–25600). Tests: Task 33 (pre-check and cleanup by prefix), Task 36 (`docker ps`/`docker network ls` before and after identical outside `mcsm-test-*`).

## Decisions resolved with the real code

- **Anchors.** The `plan-9` anchors already exist in `agent.proto` (oneof and messages), `migrations.ts`, `app.ts`, `agents/service.ts`, `app.go` (`Handle` and `Run`), `router.tsx`, `server.tsx` (two), `spec.go` and `shared/spec.ts`. Field 24 (`Event`) goes **under the `plan-9` anchor of the oneof** (the order of numbers does not matter in protobuf). As this is the last plan, edits outside an anchor are allowed and are marked «(outside anchor)».
- **`Event` (agent → panel, id = 0).** It leaves through the agent's own FIFO queue (`App.events`, the same `outbox` type as `backups.go`), not through the session's `send`: whoever emits it (the Docker event loop, the reconciler with its lock held) never blocks. Without a connection it is lost: what matters (a latched server) is also in `Observed.error`. Types: `crash`, `oom`, `crash_loop`, `crash_loop_failed`, `drift`, `save_on_pending`, `save_on_resumed`; the panel adds `token_rotated`. Levels `info|warn|error`.
- **Crash loop (§10).** One crash is counted for each `die` event of a server container with `exitCode` ≠ 0 (a Docker attribute, which the existing `Moby.Events` already copies into `Event.Labels`) that does not explain a stop requested in the last 3 min (`crashloop.Intents`, fed by a wrapper of `docker.Engine` that annotates each `Stop`/`Kill`/`Remove`, and by the router proxy when it lets a stop through) nor a reservation (`rec.Held`). More than 3 in one hour (`MaxCrashes = 3`, one-hour window) → `rec.CrashLoopStop`: it saves the latch `{version, crashes, at}` in bbolt (bucket `crash_latch`), stops the container (also one in `restarting`) and publishes `Observed` with the error «Stopped by crash loop…». While the spec stays in that version, reconciliation **does not start it** (even if `SyncSpecs` forwards «running»); `Power` start/restart deletes the latch and restarts the count; a new version invalidates it. The agent's desired state is not touched (the panel decides it); the panel, on receiving `crash_loop`, sets `servers.desired_state = 'stopped'`. With the router's autoscale, the proxy denies mc-router starting a latched server.
- **Drift.** A container with a `mcsm.spec_hash` different from the expected one when the applied version is already the spec's version (someone changed it outside the agent, or the agent was updated) is recreated as usual and also emits `Event{kind: drift}` (notice). It is not emitted for normal recreations (new version).
- **No `SubscribeEvents`.** The spec (§5.3) cites `SubscribeEvents{}`; it is not needed: `Event`s are unsolicited (id = 0), like `RouterObserved`, and the panel stores all of them.
- **`StreamDropped` (§5.2).** The agent already counts the lines dropped per subscriber (`logs.Subscription.Dropped`); every 5 s it sends the difference as `StreamDropped{count}` on the stream's `stream_id`. The panel forwards it to the browsers of the server as the `log_dropped` that the console already shows.
- **Token rotation (§9, L5 of Plan 1).** `POST /api/nodes/:id/rotate-token` requires the node to be connected, generates a token, saves its hash in `nodes.pending_token_hash` (+ `pending_token_at`) **before** sending `RotateToken{token}`. The agent saves the token in bbolt, replies Ack and closes the session; `conn.Client` reconnects with the new token (`SetToken`). WS authentication accepts the current hash **or** the pending one; the first connection with the pending one promotes it (and the old one stops working) and records `token_rotated`. Without an Ack due to timeout/disconnection the pending one is kept (the agent may have saved it); `unsupported` or another error deletes it. With a pending one less than 2 min old, another rotation is 409. The route hangs off `POST /api/nodes/:id/…`, so the migration guard blocks it while the node migrates (desirable: the rotation reconnects the agent).
- **Settings (§8).** `SettingsService` (`app.settings`) reads `public_url` and `game_port_range` from the `settings` table with the environment (`PANEL_PUBLIC_URL`, `config.gamePortRange`) as default value. They are used by the enrollment command (`nodes/routes.ts`), the `Origin` check of the WS (`realtime/routes.ts`), the port assignment (`servers/service.ts`) and the migration proposal (`migration/service.ts`). The public URL is validated as an origin `http(s)://host[:port]` without a path. The range, 1024–65535, `from ≤ to`, at most 10 001 ports; changing it only affects the ports assigned afterwards. The per-node range of §4 (`nodes.settings`) is left out (see «Discarded»).
- **CurseForge API key.** `GET /api/settings/curseforge` (and `GET /api/settings`) also return `valid`: the stored one matches `CURSEFORGE_API_KEY_RE` and is ≤ 200 characters (migration 020 copied the env value without validating it). The web warns if it is not valid.
- **Password and enrollment (L3 of Plan 1).** `POST /api/auth/password {currentPassword, newPassword}` (session required, 10/min) changes the hash and closes the **other** sessions; a wrong current password is **400** (a 401 would trigger the expired-session handler in the web). `main.ts` sets an initial setup token (`PANEL_SETUP_TOKEN` or a random one that it writes to the log if the panel has no password); with it, `/api/auth/setup` requires `setupToken`. `buildApp` without `config.setupToken` (the tests) does not require it.
- **Panel security (L1, L6, L10 of Plan 1; L1 of Plan 5).** Headers on all responses (root `onSend`, registered before the routes): CSP `default-src 'self'` with `style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'` (Radix and uPlot set inline styles) and `img-src 'self' data: blob:`, `frame-ancestors 'none'`, `nosniff`, `no-referrer`, `X-Frame-Options: DENY`; if a route already set the header, it is respected. The login rate limit groups IPv6 by /64 and a global limit of 30 failures/min is added. The access log redacts `/agent/transfer/<id>`.
- **Socket proxy for mc-router (★ of Plan 8).** Package `dockerproxy`: the agent listens on `<DATA_ROOT>/.mcsm-router/docker.sock` (host path thanks to the mirror mount; owner 65532, mode 0600) and forwards to the real socket only `GET/HEAD /_ping`, `GET /version`, `GET /info`, `GET /containers/json`, `GET /events`, `GET /containers/{id}/json` (with `Config.Env` removed) and `POST /containers/{id}/start|stop` if the agent's policy allows it (server of this node, with `mc-router.host`, not reserved; to start, also not latched by a crash loop). Everything else: 403. The router mounts that socket at `/var/run/docker.sock` without `GroupAdd`. After the agent restarts (new proxy), the router that was already running is restarted once. Socket path ≤ 100 bytes (`sun_path` limit), otherwise a visible error in the router's status.
- **Pending `save-on` (m6/m7 of Plan 4).** A backup that ends with `backup.ErrResume` or a failed `save-on` when the agent starts leaves the server in the `saveon_pending` bucket; the 15 s pass of `backupService.start` retries `save-on` (except with the server reserved) until it works (`save_on_resumed`) or the server is stopped (on start, Minecraft saves again by itself).
- **Redaction of secrets in the console (L2 of Plan 3).** The log hub replaces the occurrences of `rconPassword` and `curseforgeApiKey` from the server spec with `[hidden]` (if they have ≥ 8 characters) before storing the line in the ring: it covers itzg's debug modes.
- **Agent version and hostname.** `agent/VERSION` (`0.9.0`) goes into the binary when building the image without `--build-arg VERSION` (the compose files do not pass it); `mcsm-agent -version` prints it. `HostInfo.hostname` comes from `docker info` (`Name`, the daemon's host) instead of `os.Hostname()` (the agent container's id).
- **Backup download (pending item detected: it was not on the list).** The Backups tab has the «Download» button disabled since Plan 4. `GET /api/servers/:id/backups/:backupId/download` reuses `BackupPush` (Plan 7) with an incoming transfer that is forwarded to the browser in streaming, like the Files download.
- **`router` in the web's WS (bug detected in Plan 8).** `topicOf` (`web/src/lib/ws.ts`) does not know the `router` message, so the router's status never arrived live: it is routed to the `nodes` topic.
- **E2E.** `deploy/e2e/run.sh` builds the images with the deployment compose file (`deploy/docker-compose.yml` + `deploy/e2e/compose.e2e.yml`, project `mcsm-test-e2e`), configures the panel through the API (setup token, password, range 26600–26620), enrolls node A (the compose agent, the host's Docker) and node B (`docker:29-dind` with the agent inside, `PANEL_URL` by IP) and runs Playwright in `mcr.microsoft.com/playwright:v1.64.0-noble` on the compose network. The screenshots are left in `panel/apps/e2e/screenshots/` (ignored by git). Goal < 15 min with the images cached.

## Batch 0 — Protocol

### Task 1: `Event`, `StreamDropped` and `RotateToken` in the protocol

**Agent:** mechanical-dev

**Files:**
- Modify: `proto/mcsm/v1/agent.proto` (oneof `body` and messages area, under the `// ── plan-9 ──` anchors)
- Regenerate: `agent/gen/mcsm/v1/agent.pb.go`, `panel/packages/proto-ts/src/gen/mcsm/v1/agent_pb.ts`
- Create: `panel/apps/api/src/agents/plan9-protocol.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Envelope`, `isResponseCase` (`panel/apps/api/src/agents/hub.ts`).
- Produces (Go): `mcsmv1.EventLevel` (`EventLevel_EVENT_LEVEL_UNSPECIFIED|INFO|WARN|ERROR`), `mcsmv1.Event{Level EventLevel; ServerId, Kind, Message string; TsUnixMs int64}`, `mcsmv1.StreamDropped{Count uint64}`, `mcsmv1.RotateToken{Token string}`; bodies `Envelope_Event`, `Envelope_StreamDropped`, `Envelope_RotateToken`.
- Produces (TS): cases `"event" | "streamDropped" | "rotateToken"` of `Envelope.body`; `EventLevel.INFO|WARN|ERROR`; `count`/`tsUnixMs` are `bigint`. When importing the type `Event` in the panel, use an alias (`type Event as AgentEvent`): `Event` is also a Node global.

- [ ] **Step 1: Failing test**

`panel/apps/api/src/agents/plan9-protocol.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { create, fromBinary, toBinary, type MessageInitShape } from "@bufbuild/protobuf";
import { EnvelopeSchema, EventLevel } from "@mcsm/proto";
import { isResponseCase } from "./hub.js";

const roundTrip = (init: MessageInitShape<typeof EnvelopeSchema>) =>
  fromBinary(EnvelopeSchema, toBinary(EnvelopeSchema, create(EnvelopeSchema, init)));

describe("Plan 9 protocol", () => {
  it("no new body resolves a request: RotateToken replies with Ack", () => {
    for (const c of ["event", "streamDropped", "rotateToken"]) expect(isResponseCase(c)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("Event carries level, server, kind, message and time", () => {
    const env = roundTrip({ body: { case: "event", value: { level: EventLevel.ERROR, serverId: "s1", kind: "crash_loop", message: "parado", tsUnixMs: 5n } } });
    if (env.body.case !== "event") throw new Error(`cuerpo ${env.body.case}`);
    expect(env.body.value).toMatchObject({ level: EventLevel.ERROR, serverId: "s1", kind: "crash_loop", message: "parado", tsUnixMs: 5n });
  });

  it("StreamDropped goes in the stream's stream_id and RotateToken carries the token", () => {
    const sd = roundTrip({ streamId: 9n, body: { case: "streamDropped", value: { count: 3n } } });
    expect(sd.streamId).toBe(9n);
    expect(sd.body.case === "streamDropped" && sd.body.value.count).toBe(3n);
    const rt = roundTrip({ id: 4n, body: { case: "rotateToken", value: { token: "x".repeat(43) } } });
    expect(rt.body.case === "rotateToken" && rt.body.value.token).toBe("x".repeat(43));
  });
});
```

At the end of the file, under the last line `// ── plan-9 ──`:
```proto
// UI tests and polish (spec §5.2, §5.3, §9, §10).

enum EventLevel {
  EVENT_LEVEL_UNSPECIFIED = 0;
  EVENT_LEVEL_INFO = 1;
  EVENT_LEVEL_WARN = 2;
  EVENT_LEVEL_ERROR = 3;
}

// Agent → panel, unsolicited (id = 0): an incident of a server or of the node. It leaves through a queue of its own
// in the agent that never blocks whoever emits it; without a connection it is lost (what matters, e.g. a server
// stopped by a crash loop, is also visible in Observed.error). kind:
//   crash              the server closed with a code ≠ 0 without anyone requesting it
//   oom                Docker killed it for lack of memory
//   crash_loop         more than 3 crashes in one hour: the agent stopped it and will not start it until a Power
//                      start/restart or a new version of the spec (the panel sets the desired state to stopped)
//   crash_loop_failed  crash loop, but it could not be stopped (the message says why)
//   drift              the container did not match the already applied spec and was recreated
//   save_on_pending    autosave may have been left disabled (save-on failed); the agent retries it
//   save_on_resumed    save-on works again
message Event {
  EventLevel level = 1;
  string server_id = 2; // empty = node incident
  string kind = 3;
  string message = 4; // in Spanish, to be shown as is
  int64 ts_unix_ms = 5;
}

// Agent → panel, on a log stream (stream_id of SubscribeLogs): the agent dropped `count` new lines of that stream
// because they were not read in time (spec §5.2). The panel warns the browsers with log_dropped.
message StreamDropped {
  uint64 count = 1;
}

// Panel → agent: new permanent node token (spec §9). The agent saves it and replies Ack; afterwards it closes
// the session and reconnects with it. Error: invalid_request (format) | io_error (could not be saved). An agent
// prior to Plan 9 replies unsupported and keeps the token it had.
message RotateToken {
  string token = 1; // 32 random bytes in base64url without padding (43 characters)
}
```

```go
func TestCrashLatchRoundTripSurvivesReopen(t *testing.T) {
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`agent/internal/store/crash.go`:
```go
package store

import (
	"encoding/binary"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"sort"
	"time"

	"go.etcd.io/bbolt"
)

// plan-9: retention for crash loops (spec §10) and servers with pending save-on (m6/m7 of Plan 4).
// The buckets are created on first use, as router and tombstones are, so the Open list is not touched.
var (
	bCrashLatch = []byte("crash_latch")
	bSaveOn     = []byte("saveon_pending")
)

// CrashLatch marks a server stopped by a crash loop. While its spec stays at Version, reconciliation
// does not start it; a power action (Power start/restart) or a new version of the spec lifts it.
type CrashLatch struct {
	Version uint64    `json:"version"`
	Crashes int       `json:"crashes"`
	At      time.Time `json:"at"`
}

// PutCrashLatch saves (or replaces) the retention of id.
func (s *Store) PutCrashLatch(id string, l CrashLatch) error {
	if id == "" {
		return errors.New("store: retention without server_id")
	}
	raw, err := json.Marshal(l)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return s.db.Update(func(tx *bbolt.Tx) error {
		b, err := tx.CreateBucketIfNotExists(bCrashLatch)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return b.Put([]byte(id), raw)
	})
}

// CrashLatch returns the retention of id, or nil if it has none.
func (s *Store) CrashLatch(id string) (*CrashLatch, error) {
	var out *CrashLatch
	err := s.db.View(func(tx *bbolt.Tx) error {
		b := tx.Bucket(bCrashLatch)
		if b == nil {
			return nil
		}
		raw := b.Get([]byte(id))
		if raw == nil {
			return nil
		}
		var l CrashLatch
		if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &l); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		out = &l
		return nil
	})
	return out, err
}

// DeleteCrashLatch deletes the retention of id (idempotent).
func (s *Store) DeleteCrashLatch(id string) error {
	return s.db.Update(func(tx *bbolt.Tx) error { return deleteCrashLatch(tx, id) })
}

func deleteCrashLatch(tx *bbolt.Tx, id string) error {
	if b := tx.Bucket(bCrashLatch); b != nil {
		return b.Delete([]byte(id))
	}
	return nil
}

// MarkSaveOnPending notes that the autosave of id may have been left disabled (save-on failed).
func (s *Store) MarkSaveOnPending(id string, at time.Time) error {
	if id == "" {
		return errors.New("store: save-on pendiente sin server_id")
	}
	buf := make([]byte, 8)
	binary.BigEndian.PutUint64(buf, uint64(at.UnixMilli()))
	return s.db.Update(func(tx *bbolt.Tx) error {
		b, err := tx.CreateBucketIfNotExists(bSaveOn)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return b.Put([]byte(id), buf)
	})
}

// SaveOnPending lists, sorted, the servers with pending save-on.
func (s *Store) SaveOnPending() ([]string, error) {
	var ids []string
	err := s.db.View(func(tx *bbolt.Tx) error {
		b := tx.Bucket(bSaveOn)
		if b == nil {
			return nil
		}
		return b.ForEach(func(k, _ []byte) error {
			ids = append(ids, string(k))
			return nil
		})
	})
	sort.Strings(ids)
	return ids, err
}

// ClearSaveOnPending removes id from the pending ones (idempotent).
func (s *Store) ClearSaveOnPending(id string) error {
	return s.db.Update(func(tx *bbolt.Tx) error { return deleteSaveOn(tx, id) })
}

func deleteSaveOn(tx *bbolt.Tx, id string) error {
	if b := tx.Bucket(bSaveOn); b != nil {
		return b.Delete([]byte(id))
	}
	return nil
}
```

In `agent/internal/store/store.go`, `DeleteSpec` becomes:
```go
func (s *Store) DeleteSpec(id string) error {
	return s.db.Update(func(tx *bbolt.Tx) error {
		if err := tx.Bucket(bSpecs).Delete([]byte(id)); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := tx.Bucket(bApplied).Delete([]byte(id)); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		// plan-9: without a spec, their crash-loop retention and their pending save-on are also dropped.
		if err := deleteCrashLatch(tx, id); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return deleteSaveOn(tx, id)
	})
}
```

```go
package crashloop

import (
	"context"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/moby/moby/api/types/container"

	"mcsm/agent/internal/docker"
)

type clock struct{ t time.Time }

func (c *clock) now() time.Time { return c.t }

func TestTrackerTripsOnFourthCrashWithinAnHour(t *testing.T) {
	c := &clock{t: time.Unix(1_000_000, 0)}
	tr := NewTracker(c.now)
	for i := 1; i <= MaxCrashes; i++ {
		if n, tripped := tr.Record("a"); n != i || tripped {
			t.Fatalf("crash %d: n=%d tripped=%v", i, n, tripped)
		}
		c.t = c.t.Add(10 * time.Minute)
	}
	if n, tripped := tr.Record("a"); n != MaxCrashes+1 || !tripped {
		t.Fatalf("the fourth in an hour trips: n=%d tripped=%v", n, tripped)
	}
	if n, tripped := tr.Record("a"); n != 1 || tripped {
		t.Fatalf("after tripping the count starts from zero: n=%d tripped=%v", n, tripped)
	}
	if n, _ := tr.Record("b"); n != 1 {
		t.Fatal("cada servidor cuenta aparte")
	}
}

func TestTrackerForgetsCrashesOlderThanTheWindow(t *testing.T) {
	c := &clock{t: time.Unix(1_000_000, 0)}
	tr := NewTracker(c.now)
	for i := 0; i < MaxCrashes; i++ {
		tr.Record("a")
		c.t = c.t.Add(25 * time.Minute) // 0, 25, 50 min
	}
	// 75 min: the one at 0 min no longer counts → 3 in the window, does not trip.
	if n, tripped := tr.Record("a"); n != MaxCrashes || tripped {
		t.Fatalf("n=%d tripped=%v", n, tripped)
	}
	tr.Reset("a")
	if n, _ := tr.Record("a"); n != 1 {
		t.Fatal("Reset olvida los crashes")
	}
}

func TestIntentsExpireAfterTTL(t *testing.T) {
	c := &clock{t: time.Unix(1_000_000, 0)}
	in := NewIntents(c.now)
	if in.Expected("c1") {
		t.Fatal("sin parada pedida")
	}
	in.Note("c1")
	in.Note("") // ignorado
	c.t = c.t.Add(IntentTTL)
	if !in.Expected("c1") {
		t.Fatal("within the deadline it counts as requested")
	}
	c.t = c.t.Add(time.Second)
	if in.Expected("c1") {
		t.Fatal("after the deadline it no longer does")
	}
}

func TestEngineNotesStopKillAndRemove(t *testing.T) {
	ctx := context.Background()
	fake := docker.NewFake()
	in := NewIntents(time.Now)
	eng := Engine{Engine: fake, Intents: in}
	mk := func(name string) string {
		id, err := fake.Create(ctx, name, &container.Config{Labels: map[string]string{"mcsm.managed": "true"}}, &container.HostConfig{}, nil)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		if err := fake.Start(ctx, id); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		return id
	}
	a, b, c := mk("mcsm-a"), mk("mcsm-b"), mk("mcsm-c")
	if err := eng.Stop(ctx, a, time.Second); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := eng.Kill(ctx, b); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := eng.Remove(ctx, c); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, id := range []string{a, b, c} {
		if !in.Expected(id) {
			t.Fatalf("%s: the requested stop must be noted", id)
		}
	}
	// The rest is passed as is to the engine underneath.
	if v, _ := eng.Version(ctx); v != "fake-29.0" {
		t.Fatalf("version = %q", v)
	}
}

func TestExitCode(t *testing.T) {
	for _, tc := range []struct {
		labels map[string]string
		code   int
		ok     bool
	}{
		{map[string]string{"exitCode": "137"}, 137, true},
		{map[string]string{"exitCode": "0"}, 0, true},
		{map[string]string{"exitCode": "x"}, 0, false},
		{map[string]string{}, 0, false},
	} {
		code, ok := ExitCode(docker.Event{Action: "die", Labels: tc.labels})
		if code != tc.code || ok != tc.ok {
			t.Fatalf("%v → %d %v", tc.labels, code, ok)
		}
	}
}

func TestFakeCrash(t *testing.T) {

	fake := docker.NewFake()
	id, _ := fake.Create(ctx, "mcsm-a", &container.Config{Labels: map[string]string{"mcsm.managed": "true"}}, &container.HostConfig{}, nil)
	_ = fake.Start(ctx, id)
	fake.Crash(id, 1)
	ins, err := fake.Inspect(ctx, id)
	if err != nil || ins.State != "exited" || ins.ExitCode != 1 || ins.Running {
		t.Fatalf("inspect = %+v %v", ins, err)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`agent/internal/crashloop/crashloop.go`:
```go
// Package crashloop detects crash loops of the servers (spec §10): a server that closes with an error
// more than MaxCrashes times in Window is stopped. Intents tells apart the stops that someone requested (the agent
// when stopping, recreating or removing; mc-router through the socket proxy) from the crashes.
package crashloop

import (
	"context"
	"strconv"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"mcsm/agent/internal/docker"
)

const (
	// Window is the window in which crashes are counted.
	Window = time.Hour
	// MaxCrashes: with more than this many crashes in Window the server is in a loop.
	MaxCrashes = 3
	// IntentTTL: a die up to this long after a requested Stop/Kill/Remove is not a crash. It covers the wait
	// for a clean stop (reconcile.StopTimeout, 60 s) with a margin.
	IntentTTL = 3 * time.Minute
)

// Tracker counts the crashes of each server in the last Window. Safe for concurrent use.
type Tracker struct {
	mu   sync.Mutex
	now  func() time.Time
	hits map[string][]time.Time
}

// NewTracker creates a counter with the given clock (time.Now if nil).
func NewTracker(now func() time.Time) *Tracker {
	if now == nil {
		now = time.Now
	}
	return &Tracker{now: now, hits: map[string][]time.Time{}}
}

// Record notes a crash of serverID and returns how many it has in the last Window and whether it exceeds MaxCrashes. When
// it trips it forgets the previous ones: after the stop, the server's count starts from zero.
func (t *Tracker) Record(serverID string) (count int, tripped bool) {
	t.mu.Lock()
	defer t.mu.Unlock()
	now := t.now()
	var keep []time.Time
	for _, h := range t.hits[serverID] {
		if now.Sub(h) < Window {
			keep = append(keep, h)
		}
	}
	keep = append(keep, now)
	if len(keep) > MaxCrashes {
		delete(t.hits, serverID)
		return len(keep), true
	}
	t.hits[serverID] = keep
	return len(keep), false
}

// Reset forgets the crashes of serverID (e.g. when it is started by hand).
func (t *Tracker) Reset(serverID string) {
	t.mu.Lock()
	defer t.mu.Unlock()
	delete(t.hits, serverID)
}

// Intents remembers, per container, the last requested stop (Stop, Kill, Remove). Safe for concurrent use.
type Intents struct {
	mu  sync.Mutex
	now func() time.Time
	at  map[string]time.Time
}

// NewIntents creates the registry with the given clock (time.Now if nil).
func NewIntents(now func() time.Time) *Intents {
	if now == nil {
		now = time.Now
	}
	return &Intents{now: now, at: map[string]time.Time{}}
}

// Note notes that someone requested to stop (or kill, or remove) the container id. It prunes expired notes.
func (i *Intents) Note(id string) {
	if id == "" {
		return
	}
	i.mu.Lock()
	defer i.mu.Unlock()
	now := i.now()
	for k, at := range i.at {
		if now.Sub(at) > IntentTTL {
			delete(i.at, k)
		}
	}
	i.at[id] = now
}

// Expected reports whether a die of id is explained by a stop requested at most IntentTTL ago.
func (i *Intents) Expected(id string) bool {
	i.mu.Lock()
	defer i.mu.Unlock()
	at, ok := i.at[id]
	if !ok {
		return false
	}
	if i.now().Sub(at) > IntentTTL {
		delete(i.at, id)
		return false
	}
	return true
}

// Engine wraps a docker.Engine and notes in Intents each stop, kill or removal the agent requests. Everything
// else passes to the engine underneath.
type Engine struct {
	docker.Engine
	Intents *Intents
}

func (e Engine) Stop(ctx context.Context, id string, timeout time.Duration) error {
	e.Intents.Note(id)
	return e.Engine.Stop(ctx, id, timeout)
}

func (e Engine) Kill(ctx context.Context, id string) error {
	e.Intents.Note(id)
	return e.Engine.Kill(ctx, id)
}

func (e Engine) Remove(ctx context.Context, id string) error {
	e.Intents.Note(id)
	return e.Engine.Remove(ctx, id)
}

// ExitCode returns the exit code of a die event (Docker's exitCode attribute, which Moby.Events copies into
// Event.Labels). false if it does not carry it or it is not a number.
func ExitCode(ev docker.Event) (int, bool) {
	v, ok := ev.Labels["exitCode"]
	if !ok {
		return 0, false
	}
	n, err := strconv.Atoi(v)
	return n, err == nil
}
```

**Agent:** systems-expert

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `store.CrashLatch`, `(*store.Store).PutCrashLatch/CrashLatch/DeleteCrashLatch` (Task 2).
- Produces:
  - `type EventSink func(serverID, kind, message string)`; `func (r *Reconciler) SetEventSink(fn EventSink)`; `const KindDrift = "drift"`.
  - `func (r *Reconciler) CrashLoopStop(ctx context.Context, serverID string, crashes int) error` — `Busy(reason)` if it is reserved.
  - `func (r *Reconciler) StartBlocked(serverID string) string` — "" if nothing prevents starting it; otherwise, the reason (reservation, retention or unknown server).
  - `func CrashLoopMessage(crashes int) string` — text of the visible error.
  - Behaviour: with a retention for the current version, `reconcileLocked` does not start it (and stops it if it is active); `observeLocked` shows `CrashLoopMessage` if there is no other error; `Power` start/restart deletes the retention; a new version invalidates it.

```go
type sinkRec struct {

	kinds []string
}

func (s *sinkRec) fn(_, kind, _ string) {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	s.kinds = append(s.kinds, kind)
}

func (s *sinkRec) list() []string {
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	return append([]string(nil), s.kinds...)
}
```

`agent/internal/reconcile/crashloop.go`:
```go
package reconcile

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"

	"mcsm/agent/internal/docker"
	"mcsm/agent/internal/store"
)

// EventSink receives incidents from the reconciler (plan-9: drift). It is invoked with the server's lock held: it
// must not block or call the reconciler (App connects it to its event queue, which never blocks).
type EventSink func(serverID, kind, message string)

// KindDrift: the container did not match the spec already applied and has been recreated.
const KindDrift = "drift"

// SetEventSink sets the incident receiver. It is called before launching goroutines (App.New).
func (r *Reconciler) SetEventSink(fn EventSink) { r.events = fn }

// CrashLoopMessage is the visible error (Observed.error) of a server retained by a crash loop.
func CrashLoopMessage(crashes int) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("Stopped by crash loop: it closed with an error %d times in less than an hour. Check the console and press Start to try again", crashes)
}

// crashLatched returns the retention of serverID if it belongs to this version of its spec. A retention for another
// version (a new spec arrived) is deleted. With the server's lock held.
func (r *Reconciler) crashLatched(serverID string, version uint64) (*store.CrashLatch, error) {
	l, err := r.st.CrashLatch(serverID)
	if err != nil || l == nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if l.Version != version {
		return nil, r.st.DeleteCrashLatch(serverID)
	}
	return l, nil
}

// CrashLoopStop stops a server in a crash loop (spec §10) and retains it at the current version of its spec: the
// reconciliation does not start it again (even if SyncSpecs forwards «running») until a Power start/restart or a
// new version. The desired state does not change (the panel decides it). Busy if the server is reserved.
func (r *Reconciler) CrashLoopStop(ctx context.Context, serverID string, crashes int) error {
	unlock := r.lock(serverID)
	defer unlock()
	if reason := r.heldReason(serverID); reason != "" {
		return Busy(reason)
	}
	sp, err := r.st.GetSpec(serverID)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if sp == nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("unknown server %s", serverID)
	}
	if err := r.st.PutCrashLatch(serverID, store.CrashLatch{Version: sp.Version, Crashes: crashes, At: r.now()}); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	var stopErr error
	c, err := r.find(ctx, serverID)
	if err != nil {
		stopErr = err
	} else if c != nil && isActive(c.State) { // also «restarting»: the restart policy is bringing it back up
		if err := r.eng.Stop(ctx, c.ID, StopTimeout); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, docker.ErrNotFound) {
			stopErr = fmt.Errorf("stop: %w", err)
		}
	}
	obs := r.observeLocked(ctx, serverID)
	if stopErr != nil {
		obs.Error = stopErr.Error()
	}
	r.observer(serverID, obs)
	return stopErr
}

// StartBlocked is the reason why nobody (mc-router, through the socket proxy) must start the server now: a
// reservation (backup, restore, migration…), the crash-loop retention, or the agent not knowing it. "" if it can
// start. Without a lock: it is a snapshot, like the one whoever starts it would see.
func (r *Reconciler) StartBlocked(serverID string) string {
	if reason := r.heldReason(serverID); reason != "" {
		return Busy(reason).Error()
	}
	sp, err := r.st.GetSpec(serverID)
	if err != nil {
		return err.Error()
	}
	if sp == nil {
		return "unknown server on this node"
	}
	if l, err := r.st.CrashLatch(serverID); err == nil && l != nil && l.Version == sp.Version {
		return CrashLoopMessage(l.Crashes)
	}
	return ""
}
```

```go
package app

import (
	"context"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	mcsmv1 "mcsm/agent/gen/mcsm/v1"
	"mcsm/agent/internal/crashloop"
	"mcsm/agent/internal/docker"
	"mcsm/agent/internal/spec"
)

// eventsOf drains the event queue (FIFO mark) and returns the Events that reached the sink.
func eventsOf(t *testing.T, a *App, s *sink) []*mcsmv1.Event {
	t.Helper()
	mark := make(chan struct{})
	a.events.ch <- outItem{mark: mark}
	select {
	case <-mark:
	case <-time.After(3 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("the event queue does not advance")
	}
	s.mu.Lock()
	defer s.mu.Unlock()
	var out []*mcsmv1.Event
	for _, e := range s.envs {
		if ev := e.GetEvent(); ev != nil {
			out = append(out, ev)
		}
	}
	return out
}

// crashApp: agent with server "a" running and its event queue working.
func crashApp(t *testing.T) (*App, *docker.Fake, *sink, string) {
	t.Helper()
	a, fake, _ := newApp(t)
	s := newSink()
	s.ch = make(chan *mcsmv1.Envelope, 1024)
	a.send = s.send
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	t.Cleanup(cancel)
	a.life = ctx
	go a.events.run(ctx)
	a.Handle(context.Background(), &mcsmv1.Envelope{Id: 1, Body: &mcsmv1.Envelope_ApplySpec{ApplySpec: &mcsmv1.ApplySpec{Server: specMsg("a", "alpha", 1)}}}, s.send)
	cid := a.rec.ContainerID(context.Background(), "a")
	if cid == "" {
		t.Fatal("sin contenedor")
	}
	return a, fake, s, cid
}

func die(cid string, code string) docker.Event {
	return docker.Event{Action: "die", ContainerID: cid, Labels: map[string]string{spec.LabelServerID: "a", "exitCode": code}}
}

func TestOnDieIgnoresCleanExitsRequestedStopsAndHeldServers(t *testing.T) {
	a, _, s, cid := crashApp(t)
	a.onDie("a", die(cid, "0"))                                          // clean exit (/stop, mc-router stop for inactivity)
	a.onDie("a", docker.Event{Action: "die", ContainerID: cid, Labels: map[string]string{}}) // no exitCode
	a.intents.Note(cid)
	a.onDie("a", die(cid, "143")) // stop requested by the agent
	release, err := a.rec.Hold("a", "backup")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	a.onDie("a", docker.Event{Action: "die", ContainerID: "otro", Labels: map[string]string{spec.LabelServerID: "a", "exitCode": "1"}})
	release(context.Background())
	a.onDie("other-agent", die("foreign", "1")) // server this agent does not know
	if evs := eventsOf(t, a, s); len(evs) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("eventos = %+v", evs)
	}
}

func TestFourCrashesInAnHourStopAndRetainTheServer(t *testing.T) {
	a, fake, s, cid := crashApp(t)
	for i := 0; i < crashloop.MaxCrashes+1; i++ {
		a.onDie("a", die(cid, "1"))
	}
	waitBackground(t, a)
	evs := eventsOf(t, a, s)
	var crashes, loops int
	for _, e := range evs {
		switch e.Kind {
		case KindCrash:
			crashes++
			if e.Level != mcsmv1.EventLevel_EVENT_LEVEL_WARN || e.ServerId != "a" || !strings.Contains(e.Message, "code 1") {
				t.Fatalf("crash = %+v", e)
			}
		case KindCrashLoop:
			loops++
			if e.Level != mcsmv1.EventLevel_EVENT_LEVEL_ERROR || !strings.Contains(e.Message, "has been stopped") {
				t.Fatalf("crash_loop = %+v", e)
			}
		}
	}
	if crashes != crashloop.MaxCrashes+1 || loops != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("crashes=%d loops=%d: %+v", crashes, loops, evs)
	}
	ins, err := fake.Inspect(context.Background(), cid)
	if err != nil || ins.Running {
		t.Fatalf("the server must end up stopped: %+v %v", ins, err)
	}
	if l, _ := a.st.CrashLatch("a"); l == nil || l.Crashes != crashloop.MaxCrashes+1 {
		t.Fatalf("retention = %+v", l)
	}

	// Starting it by hand lifts the retention and restarts the count.
	a.Handle(context.Background(), &mcsmv1.Envelope{Id: 9, Body: &mcsmv1.Envelope_PowerAction{PowerAction: &mcsmv1.PowerAction{ServerId: "a", Action: mcsmv1.PowerActionType_POWER_ACTION_TYPE_START}}}, s.send)
	if l, _ := a.st.CrashLatch("a"); l != nil {
		t.Fatal("Power start deletes the retention")
	}
	if n, _ := a.crashes.Record("a"); n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("the count starts from zero: %d", n)
	}
}

func TestDriftReachesTheEventQueue(t *testing.T) {
	a, fake, s, _ := crashApp(t)
	for _, c := range fake.Containers {
		c.Config.Labels[spec.LabelHash] = "changed-by-hand"
	}
	if err := a.rec.Reconcile(context.Background(), "a"); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	evs := eventsOf(t, a, s)
	if len(evs) != 1 || evs[0].Kind != "drift" || evs[0].Level != mcsmv1.EventLevel_EVENT_LEVEL_WARN {
		t.Fatalf("eventos = %+v", evs)
	}
}
```

```go
package app

import (
	"fmt"
	"time"

	mcsmv1 "mcsm/agent/gen/mcsm/v1"
	"mcsm/agent/internal/crashloop"
	"mcsm/agent/internal/docker"
)

// Event types emitted by the agent (see the comment of `message Event` in agent.proto). The drift is emitted by the
// reconciler (reconcile.KindDrift).
const (
	KindCrash           = "crash"
	KindOOM             = "oom"
	KindCrashLoop       = "crash_loop"
	KindCrashLoopFailed = "crash_loop_failed"
	KindSaveOnPending   = "save_on_pending"
	KindSaveOnResumed   = "save_on_resumed"
)

// emitEvent queues an Event for the panel without ever blocking (own FIFO queue, like the backup notices). Without a
// connection it is lost: what matters is also in Observed.error.
func (a *App) emitEvent(serverID string, level mcsmv1.EventLevel, kind, msg string) {
	_ = a.events.post(&mcsmv1.Envelope{Body: &mcsmv1.Envelope_Event{Event: &mcsmv1.Event{
		Level: level, ServerId: serverID, Kind: kind, Message: validUTF8(msg), TsUnixMs: time.Now().UnixMilli(),
	}}})
}

// onDie counts the error exits that nobody requested (spec §10). They are not crashes: a clean exit (code 0:
// /stop, the mc-router stop for inactivity), a stop that the agent or the router's proxy requested in the last few
// minutes (Intents), nothing while the server is reserved (backup, restore, migration), nor the containers of servers
// this agent does not know. With more than crashloop.MaxCrashes in one hour it stops and retains the server in the
// background, with the lifetime context.
func (a *App) onDie(serverID string, ev docker.Event) {
	code, ok := crashloop.ExitCode(ev)
	if !ok || code == 0 || a.intents.Expected(ev.ContainerID) || a.rec.Held(serverID) {
		return
	}
	if sp, err := a.st.GetSpec(serverID); err != nil || sp == nil {
		return // container of another agent on the same Docker (e.g. the tests): not ours
	}
	n, tripped := a.crashes.Record(serverID)
	a.emitEvent(serverID, mcsmv1.EventLevel_EVENT_LEVEL_WARN, KindCrash,
		fmt.Sprintf("The server closed with code %d (%d in the last hour)", code, n))
	if !tripped {
		return
	}
	life := a.life
	a.dispatch(life, "crashloop:"+serverID, func() {
		if err := a.rec.CrashLoopStop(life, serverID, n); err != nil {
			a.log.Warn("stop a server in crash loop", "server", serverID, "err", err)
			a.emitEvent(serverID, mcsmv1.EventLevel_EVENT_LEVEL_ERROR, KindCrashLoopFailed,
				fmt.Sprintf("Crash loop (%d crashes with an error in one hour), but it could not be stopped: %v", n, err))
			return
		}
		a.log.Warn("server stopped by crash loop", "server", serverID, "crashes", n)
		a.emitEvent(serverID, mcsmv1.EventLevel_EVENT_LEVEL_ERROR, KindCrashLoop,
			fmt.Sprintf("Crash loop: %d crashes with an error in less than an hour. The server has been stopped; check the console and press Start to try again", n))
	})
}
```

In `agent/internal/app/app.go`, replace `pumpLogs` entirely with:
```go
// pumpLogs forwards the lines of sub until ctx ends or the follower closes, and every droppedEvery it warns the
// panel of the lines dropped for being slow (StreamDropped, spec §5.2).
func (a *App) pumpLogs(ctx context.Context, streamID uint64, serverID string, sub *logs.Subscription, send func(*mcsmv1.Envelope) error) {
	tick := time.NewTicker(droppedEvery)
	defer tick.Stop()
	a.pump(ctx, streamID, serverID, sub.Lines, sub.Dropped, tick.C, send)
}

// pump is pumpLogs with its sources injectable (tests): the lines, the cumulative drop counter and the tick.
func (a *App) pump(ctx context.Context, streamID uint64, serverID string, lines <-chan logs.Line, dropped func() uint64, tick <-chan time.Time, send func(*mcsmv1.Envelope) error) {
	var reported uint64
	for {
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return
		case <-tick:
			if d := dropped(); d > reported {
				_ = send(&mcsmv1.Envelope{StreamId: streamID, Body: &mcsmv1.Envelope_StreamDropped{StreamDropped: &mcsmv1.StreamDropped{Count: d - reported}}})
				a.log.Warn("log lines dropped by a slow subscriber", "server", serverID, "stream", streamID, "nuevas", d-reported, "total", d)
				reported = d
			}
		case line, ok := <-lines:
			if !ok {
				return
			}
			_ = send(&mcsmv1.Envelope{StreamId: streamID, Body: &mcsmv1.Envelope_LogLine{LogLine: &mcsmv1.LogLine{
				ServerId: serverID, TsUnixMs: line.TS.UnixMilli(), Line: validUTF8(line.Text),
			}}})
		}
	}
}
```

```go
func TestSetTokenAndReconnectDialWithTheNewToken(t *testing.T) {
```

```go
}

func TestRotateTokenSavesBeforeAckAndReconnectsAfter(t *testing.T) {
```

```go
// SetToken changes the node token for the following sessions (plan-9: RotateToken). It does not touch the current one.
func (c *Client) SetToken(tok string) {
	c.tokMu.Lock()
	c.tok = tok
	c.tokMu.Unlock()
}

func (c *Client) token() string {
	c.tokMu.Lock()
	defer c.tokMu.Unlock()
	return c.tok
}

// Reconnect closes the current session, if there is one: Run reconnects (with the current token) after the minimum wait.
func (c *Client) Reconnect() {
	c.mu.Lock()
	ws := c.ws
	c.mu.Unlock()
	if ws != nil {
		_ = ws.Close(websocket.StatusNormalClosure, "token rotado")
	}
}
```
4. In `session`, the header becomes `HTTPHeader: http.Header{"Authorization": []string{"Bearer " + c.token()}},`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the agent**

`agent/internal/app/rotate.go`:
```go
package app

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"regexp"

	mcsmv1 "mcsm/agent/gen/mcsm/v1"
	"mcsm/agent/internal/errcode"
	"mcsm/agent/internal/store"
)

// nodeTokenRe: the permanent token generated by the panel (randomToken: 32 bytes in base64url, 43 characters).
var nodeTokenRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$`)

// handleRotateToken (spec §9) saves the new token BEFORE replying and, after the Ack, closes the session to
// reconnect with it. If the Ack is lost, the panel keeps the token as pending and accepts it on the reconnection.
// Transfers read the token from the store on each one (App.transfers): they use the new one from then on.
func (a *App) handleRotateToken(id uint64, req *mcsmv1.RotateToken, send func(*mcsmv1.Envelope) error) {
	tok := req.GetToken()
	if !nodeTokenRe.MatchString(tok) {
		_ = send(errMsg(id, errcode.InvalidRequest, errors.New("node token with invalid format")))
		return
	}
	creds, err := a.st.Credentials()
	if err == nil && creds == nil {
		err = errors.New("the node has no saved credentials")
	}
	if err != nil {
		_ = send(errMsg(id, errcode.IOError, fmt.Errorf("read the node's credentials: %w", err)))
		return
	}
	if err := a.st.SaveCredentials(store.Credentials{NodeID: creds.NodeID, Token: tok}); err != nil {
		_ = send(errMsg(id, errcode.IOError, fmt.Errorf("save the new token: %w", err)))
		return
	}
	a.setToken(tok)
	_ = send(ack(id))
	a.log.Info("node token rotated: reconnecting with the new one")
	a.reconnect()
}
```


```go
	redacted     = "[hidden]"
	minSecretLen = 8 // shorter ones would hide normal text
```
2. Field in `Hub`, after `grace`: `secrets func(serverID string) []string // plan-9: what must not appear in the console`.
3. Field in `follower`, after `containerID`: `secrets []string`.
4. Methods:
```go
// SetSecrets sets where, per server, the values to hide in its logs come from (RCON password, CurseForge API key;
// L2 of Plan 3: itzg's debug modes may print them). It is queried when each follower is opened: a spec change
// recreates the container and opens another one.
func (h *Hub) SetSecrets(fn func(serverID string) []string) {
	h.mu.Lock()
	h.secrets = fn
	h.mu.Unlock()
}

func (h *Hub) secretsOf(serverID string) []string {
	h.mu.Lock()
	fn := h.secrets
	h.mu.Unlock()
	if fn == nil {
		return nil
	}
	var out []string
	for _, s := range fn(serverID) {
		if len(s) >= minSecretLen {
			out = append(out, s)
		}
	}
	return out
}

// redact replaces each occurrence of a secret with [hidden].
func redact(text string, secrets []string) string {
	for _, s := range secrets {
		if strings.Contains(text, s) {
			text = strings.ReplaceAll(text, s, redacted)
		}
	}
	return text
}
```
5. In `SubscribeSub`, inside `if f == nil {`, right after `h.mu.Unlock()`: `secrets := h.secretsOf(serverID)`; and the new follower literal adds `secrets: secrets`.
6. In `follower.run`, the publication becomes:
```go
		if line != nil {
			l := parseLine(string(line))
			l.Text = redact(l.Text, f.secrets)
			f.publish(l)
		}
```

```go
package app

import "encoding/json"

// logSecrets are the secrets of a server's spec that must not appear in its console (L2 of Plan 3). They are read
// without validating the spec: a spec that no longer validates still has to hide them.
func (a *App) logSecrets(serverID string) []string {
	sp, err := a.st.GetSpec(serverID)
	if err != nil || sp == nil {
		return nil
	}
	var s struct {
		Rcon       string `json:"rconPassword"`
		CurseForge string `json:"curseforgeApiKey"`
	}
	if json.Unmarshal(sp.SpecJson, &s) != nil {
		return nil
	}
	return []string{s.Rcon, s.CurseForge}
}
```
In `New` (`app.go`), after `a.hub = logs.NewHub(eng)`: `a.hub.SetSecrets(a.logSecrets) // plan-9`.

**Interfaces:**

- Produces: `func (b *backupService) markSaveOn(serverID string)`; `func (b *backupService) retrySaveOn(ctx context.Context)` — for each pending one: withdrawn → forgotten; reserved → skipped; stopped → forgotten (on start Minecraft saves again); running → `save-on` over RCON (15 s) and, if it works, it is forgotten and `save_on_resumed` is emitted.
```go
package app

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"slices"
	"sync/atomic"
	"testing"
	"time"

	mcsmv1 "mcsm/agent/gen/mcsm/v1"
	"mcsm/agent/internal/jobs"
)

// failingSaveOn makes save-on fail while fail is true; the rest goes to the environment's fake RCON.
func failingSaveOn(e *backupEnv, fail *atomic.Bool) {
	e.a.rcon = func(ctx context.Context, host string, port uint16, pass, cmd string) (string, error) {
		if cmd == "save-on" && fail.Load() {
			return "", errors.New("rcon down")
		}
		return e.rc.fn(ctx, host, port, pass, cmd)
	}
}

// eventKinds delivers what is in the event queue and returns the kinds received.
func eventKinds(t *testing.T, e *backupEnv) []string {
	t.Helper()
	mark := make(chan struct{})
	e.a.events.ch <- outItem{mark: mark}
	select {
	case <-mark:
	case <-time.After(3 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("the event queue does not advance")
	}
	var out []string
	for _, env := range e.col.all() {
		if ev := env.GetEvent(); ev != nil {
			out = append(out, ev.Kind)
		}
	}
	return out
}

func startEvents(t *testing.T, e *backupEnv) {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	t.Cleanup(cancel)
	go e.a.events.run(ctx)
}

func TestSaveOnFailureIsRetriedUntilItWorks(t *testing.T) {
	e := newBackupEnv(t, backupsSpecJSON)
	startEvents(t, e)
	var fail atomic.Bool
	fail.Store(true)
	failingSaveOn(e, &fail)
	if r := e.handle(2, &mcsmv1.BackupNow{ServerId: "a", JobId: "j1"}); r.GetAck() == nil {
		t.Fatalf("BackupNow: %+v", r)
	}
	e.settle(t)
	if f := e.col.final("j1"); f == nil || f.Status != mcsmv1.JobStatus_JOB_STATUS_FAILED {
		t.Fatalf("the backup with failed save-on fails: %+v", f)
	}
	if ids, _ := e.st.SaveOnPending(); !slices.Equal(ids, []string{"a"}) {
		t.Fatalf("pendientes = %v", ids)
	}
	e.a.bk.retrySaveOn(context.Background()) // still failing
	if ids, _ := e.st.SaveOnPending(); len(ids) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("pendientes = %v", ids)
	}
	fail.Store(false)
	e.a.bk.retrySaveOn(context.Background())
	if ids, _ := e.st.SaveOnPending(); len(ids) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("pendientes = %v", ids)
	}
	if got := eventKinds(t, e); !slices.Equal(got, []string{KindSaveOnPending, KindSaveOnResumed}) {
		t.Fatalf("eventos = %v", got)
	}
}

func TestSaveOnPendingForgottenWhenStoppedAndSkippedWhenHeld(t *testing.T) {
	e := newBackupEnv(t, backupsSpecJSON)
	if err := e.st.MarkSaveOnPending("a", time.Unix(1, 0)); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	release, err := e.a.rec.Hold("a", "backup")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	before := len(e.rc.list())
	e.a.bk.retrySaveOn(context.Background())
	if len(e.rc.list()) != before {
		t.Fatal("with the server reserved, RCON is not touched")
	}
	release(context.Background())
	if r := e.handle(3, &mcsmv1.PowerAction{ServerId: "a", Action: mcsmv1.PowerActionType_POWER_ACTION_TYPE_STOP}); r.GetAck() == nil {
		t.Fatalf("stop: %+v", r)
	}
	before = len(e.rc.list())
	e.a.bk.retrySaveOn(context.Background())
	if ids, _ := e.st.SaveOnPending(); len(ids) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("parado: se olvida; pendientes = %v", ids)
	}
	if len(e.rc.list()) != before {
		t.Fatal("stopped: no save-on is sent")
	}
}

func TestResumeAtStartMarksPendingWhenSaveOnFails(t *testing.T) {
	e := newBackupEnv(t, backupsSpecJSON)
	var fail atomic.Bool
	fail.Store(true)
	failingSaveOn(e, &fail)
	if err := e.st.PutJob(&mcsmv1.JobRecord{JobId: "j0", ServerId: "a", Type: jobs.TypeBackup, Status: mcsmv1.JobStatus_JOB_STATUS_RUNNING}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	e.a.bk.resumeInterrupted(context.Background())
	if ids, _ := e.st.SaveOnPending(); !slices.Equal(ids, []string{"a"}) {
		t.Fatalf("pendientes = %v", ids)
	}
}
```

4. New methods (at the end of the file, before `outbox`):
```go
// markSaveOn notes that the autosave of serverID may have been left disabled (save-on failed when a backup ended or
// when the agent started) and warns the panel. retrySaveOn retries it (m6/m7 of Plan 4).
func (b *backupService) markSaveOn(serverID string) {
	if err := b.a.st.MarkSaveOnPending(serverID, b.clock.Now()); err != nil {
		b.a.log.Error("anotar save-on pendiente", "server", serverID, "err", err)
	}
	b.a.emitEvent(serverID, mcsmv1.EventLevel_EVENT_LEVEL_WARN, KindSaveOnPending,
		"Autosave may have been left disabled (save-on failed). The agent retries it every 15 s; if you can, run save-on in the console")
}

// retrySaveOn retries save-on on the pending servers. A withdrawn or stopped server is forgotten (on start,
// Minecraft saves again by itself); a reserved one is skipped: its backup or restore manages its own save-off.
func (b *backupService) retrySaveOn(ctx context.Context) {
	ids, err := b.a.st.SaveOnPending()
	if err != nil {
		b.a.log.Warn("listar save-on pendientes", "err", err)
		return
	}
	for _, id := range ids {
		if b.a.rec.Held(id) {
			continue
		}
		t, err := b.target(id)
		if errors.Is(err, errUnknownServer) {
			_ = b.a.st.ClearSaveOnPending(id)
			continue
		}
		if err != nil {
			continue
		}
		running, err := b.isRunning(ctx, id)
		if err != nil {
			continue
		}
		if !running {
			_ = b.a.st.ClearSaveOnPending(id)
			b.a.log.Info("pending save-on forgotten: the server is stopped and will save on start", "server", t.slug)
			continue
		}
		cctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, resumeOnStartTimeout)
		_, err = b.a.rcon(cctx, spec.ContainerName(t.slug), uint16(spec.RconPort), t.spec.RconPassword, "save-on")
		cancel()
		if err != nil {
			continue
		}
		if err := b.a.st.ClearSaveOnPending(id); err != nil {
			b.a.log.Warn("borrar save-on pendiente", "server", t.slug, "err", err)
		}
		b.a.log.Info("autosave re-enabled (save-on)", "server", t.slug)
		b.a.emitEvent(id, mcsmv1.EventLevel_EVENT_LEVEL_INFO, KindSaveOnResumed, "Autosave re-enabled (save-on)")
	}
}
```

```dockerfile
# Base images pinned by digest (L11): the agent has the Docker socket. To update them:
# docker buildx imagetools inspect <image:tag> and replace the digest.
FROM golang:1.27.2-alpine@sha256:f92b6ef800e499660581efdabdf25d9d817a9d124eaf900924f0504e7e27e12d AS build
WORKDIR /src
COPY go.mod go.sum ./
RUN go mod download
COPY . .
# Empty = the version in agent/VERSION (the docker-compose files do not pass --build-arg VERSION).
ARG VERSION=
RUN v="${VERSION:-$(tr -d '[:space:]' < VERSION)}" && CGO_ENABLED=0 go build -trimpath -ldflags="-s -w -X main.version=${v}" -o /out/mcsm-agent ./cmd/agent

FROM alpine:3.22@sha256:5291449c3df73caf6ed85e649dec1b9e818b39a5d8c871e97afc13e9cd5e8fa8
RUN apk add --no-cache ca-certificates tzdata
COPY --from=build /out/mcsm-agent /usr/local/bin/mcsm-agent
# Runs as root on purpose: it needs /var/run/docker.sock and to write to DATA_ROOT
# (host bind mount, owned by root or by the itzg container's user).
ENV DATA_ROOT=/srv/minecraft AGENT_DATA=/var/lib/mcsm-agent MCSM_NETWORK=mcsm
VOLUME ["/var/lib/mcsm-agent"]
ENTRYPOINT ["mcsm-agent"]
```

```go
package dockerproxy

import (

func TestClassifyAllowList(t *testing.T) {
	allowed := []struct {
		method, path string
		op           Op
		id           string
	}{
		{"GET", "/_ping", OpPing, ""},
		{"HEAD", "/v1.47/_ping", OpPing, ""},
		{"GET", "/v1.47/version", OpVersion, ""},
		{"GET", "/v1.47/info", OpInfo, ""},
		{"GET", "/v1.47/containers/json", OpList, ""},
		{"GET", "/v1.47/events", OpEvents, ""},
		{"GET", "/v1.47/containers/abc123/json", OpInspect, "abc123"},
		{"POST", "/v1.47/containers/mcsm-alpha/start", OpStart, "mcsm-alpha"},
		{"POST", "/containers/abc123/stop", OpStop, "abc123"},
	}
	for _, c := range allowed {
		op, id, ok := Classify(c.method, c.path, "")
		if !ok || op != c.op || id != c.id {
			t.Errorf("%s %s → %v %q %v", c.method, c.path, op, id, ok)
		}
	}
	denied := []struct{ method, path, raw string }{
		{"POST", "/v1.47/containers/create", ""},
		{"POST", "/v1.47/containers/abc/exec", ""},
		{"POST", "/v1.47/containers/abc/kill", ""},
		{"POST", "/v1.47/containers/abc/restart", ""},
		{"POST", "/v1.47/containers/abc/unpause", ""},
		{"DELETE", "/v1.47/containers/abc", ""},
		{"GET", "/v1.47/containers/abc/logs", ""},
		{"GET", "/v1.47/containers/abc/archive", ""},
		{"POST", "/v1.47/containers/abc/json", ""},
		{"GET", "/v1.47/containers/abc/start", ""},
		{"GET", "/v1.47/images/json", ""},
		{"POST", "/v1.47/networks/create", ""},
		{"POST", "/v1.47/build", ""},
		{"GET", "/v1.47/containers/../json", ""},
		{"GET", "//containers/json", ""},
		{"GET", "/containers/json", "/containers%2Fjson"},
		{"GET", "/v1.47/containers/a%2Fb/json", "/v1.47/containers/a%2Fb/json"},
	}
	for _, c := range denied {
		if op, id, ok := Classify(c.method, c.path, c.raw); ok {
			t.Errorf("%s %s (raw %q) must be denied; got %v %q", c.method, c.path, c.raw, op, id)
		}
	}
}

func TestStripEnv(t *testing.T) {
	out, err := StripEnv([]byte(`{"Id":"x","Name":"/mcsm-a","Config":{"Env":["RCON_PASSWORD=secret"],"Labels":{"mc-router.host":"a.lan"}}}`))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if strings.Contains(string(out), "secret") || strings.Contains(string(out), "Env") {
		t.Fatalf("the environment remains: %s", out)
	}
	var m map[string]any
	_ = json.Unmarshal(out, &m)
	if m["Id"] != "x" || m["Config"].(map[string]any)["Labels"].(map[string]any)["mc-router.host"] != "a.lan" {
		t.Fatalf("the rest is lost: %s", out)
	}
	if out, err := StripEnv([]byte(`{"Id":"x"}`)); err != nil || string(out) != `{"Id":"x"}` {
		t.Fatalf("without Config: %s %v", out, err)
	}
	if _, err := StripEnv([]byte(`no json`)); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("an unreadable inspect is an error")
	}
}

// upstream is a fake Docker listening on a unix socket.
type upstream struct {
	mu     sync.Mutex
	got    []string
	events chan string
}

func startUpstream(t *testing.T, dir string) (*upstream, string) {
	t.Helper()
	u := &upstream{events: make(chan string, 4)}
	path := filepath.Join(dir, "docker.sock")
	ln, err := net.Listen("unix", path)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	srv := &http.Server{Handler: http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		u.mu.Lock()
		u.got = append(u.got, r.Method+" "+r.URL.Path)
		u.mu.Unlock()
		switch {
		case strings.HasSuffix(r.URL.Path, "/events"):
			w.WriteHeader(200)
			w.(http.Flusher).Flush()
			for {
				select {
				case ev := <-u.events:
					_, _ = io.WriteString(w, ev+"\n")
					w.(http.Flusher).Flush()
				case <-r.Context().Done():
					return
				}
			}
		case strings.HasSuffix(r.URL.Path, "/json") && strings.Contains(r.URL.Path, "/containers/") && !strings.HasSuffix(r.URL.Path, "/containers/json"):
			_, _ = io.WriteString(w, `{"Id":"abc","Config":{"Env":["RCON_PASSWORD=SECRET"],"Labels":{}}}`)
		default:
			w.WriteHeader(204)
		}
	})}
	go func() { _ = srv.Serve(ln) }()
	t.Cleanup(func() { _ = srv.Close() })
	return u, path
}

func (u *upstream) requests() []string {
	u.mu.Lock()
	defer u.mu.Unlock()
	return append([]string(nil), u.got...)
}

func unixClient(path string) *http.Client {
	return &http.Client{Transport: &http.Transport{DialContext: func(ctx context.Context, _, _ string) (net.Conn, error) {
		var d net.Dialer
		return d.DialContext(ctx, "unix", path)
	}}}
}

func TestProxyEndToEnd(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	up, upPath := startUpstream(t, dir)
	listen := filepath.Join(dir, "proxy", "docker.sock")
	p := New(Options{Listen: listen, Upstream: upPath, UID: -1, GID: -1, Log: slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(io.Discard, nil)),
		Policy: func(_ context.Context, op Op, c string) error {
			if c == "retained" {
				return errors.New("stopped by crash loop")
			}
			return nil
		}})
	if err := p.Ready(); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("without Listen it is not ready")
	}
	if err := p.Listen(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := p.Ready(); err != nil || p.Since().IsZero() {
		t.Fatalf("ready = %v since = %v", err, p.Since())
	}
	if fi, err := os.Stat(listen); err != nil || fi.Mode().Perm() != 0o600 {
		t.Fatalf("socket permissions = %v %v", fi.Mode(), err)
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	defer cancel()
	go p.Serve(ctx)
	c := unixClient(listen)
	do := func(method, path string) *http.Response {
		t.Helper()
		req, _ := http.NewRequest(method, "http://docker"+path, nil)
		res, err := c.Do(req)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
		return res
	}

	res := do("GET", "/v1.47/containers/abc/json")
	body, _ := io.ReadAll(res.Body)
	res.Body.Close()
	if res.StatusCode != 200 || strings.Contains(string(body), "SECRET") || !strings.Contains(string(body), `"Id":"abc"`) {
		t.Fatalf("inspect = %d %s", res.StatusCode, body)
	}
	if res := do("POST", "/v1.47/containers/create"); res.StatusCode != 403 {
		t.Fatalf("create = %d", res.StatusCode)
	}
	res = do("POST", "/v1.47/containers/retained/start")
	body, _ = io.ReadAll(res.Body)
	res.Body.Close()
	if res.StatusCode != 409 || !strings.Contains(string(body), "crash loop") {
		t.Fatalf("start retained = %d %s", res.StatusCode, body)
	}
	if res := do("POST", "/v1.47/containers/ok/start"); res.StatusCode != 204 {
		t.Fatalf("start = %d", res.StatusCode)
	}
	got := strings.Join(up.requests(), "|")
	if strings.Contains(got, "create") || strings.Contains(got, "retained") || !strings.Contains(got, "POST /v1.47/containers/ok/start") {
		t.Fatalf("reached the real Docker: %s", got)
	}

	// Events arrive as soon as they are emitted (without waiting for the end of the response).
	res = do("GET", "/v1.47/events")
	defer res.Body.Close()
	up.events <- `{"Action":"start"}`
	line, err := bufio.NewReader(res.Body).ReadString('\n')
	if err != nil || !strings.Contains(line, `"start"`) {
		t.Fatalf("event = %q %v", line, err)
	}
}

func TestListenRejectsLongPaths(t *testing.T) {
	p := New(Options{Listen: "/" + strings.Repeat("x", MaxSocketPath), Upstream: "/nada", UID: -1})
	if err := p.Listen(); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "too long") {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v", err)
	}
	if err := p.Ready(); err == nil || !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "too long") {
		t.Fatalf("ready = %v", err)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`agent/internal/dockerproxy/proxy.go`:
```go
// Package dockerproxy serves mc-router a trimmed Docker API (★ of Plan 8): only what it needs to discover the
// servers (ping, version, info, list, inspect without environment, events) and, with autoscale, to start and stop the
// servers that the agent's policy allows. Everything else (creating containers, exec, images, networks, volumes…) is
// 403: the router stops having root on the node through the socket.
package dockerproxy

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"io/fs"
	"log/slog"
	"net"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httputil"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"regexp"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"time"
)

// Op is an allowed operation of the Docker API.
type Op int

const (
	OpPing Op = iota + 1
	OpVersion
	OpInfo
	OpList
	OpInspect
	OpEvents
	OpStart
	OpStop
)

// MaxSocketPath: sun_path admits 108 bytes including the final NUL; a margin is left.
const MaxSocketPath = 100

// maxInspectBytes bounds the inspect that is rewritten (a normal one takes a few KiB).
const maxInspectBytes = 4 << 20

var (
	// prefixRe separates the optional version prefix (/v1.47) from the rest. Only simple path characters.
	prefixRe    = regexp.MustCompile(`^(/v[0-9]{1,2}\.[0-9]{1,3})?(/[A-Za-z0-9_./-]*)$`)
	containerRe = regexp.MustCompile(`^/containers/([A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,127})/(json|start|stop)$`)
)

// Classify decides whether a request is on the allowlist and which operation it is. A route with escapes (non-empty
// rawPath), «..» or «//» is denied without further checks: there is no way for two readings of the route to disagree.
func Classify(method, path, rawPath string) (op Op, container string, ok bool) {
	if rawPath != "" || strings.Contains(path, "..") || strings.Contains(path, "//") {
		return 0, "", false
	}
	m := prefixRe.FindStringSubmatch(path)
	if m == nil {
		return 0, "", false
	}
	p, get := m[2], method == http.MethodGet
	switch {
	case p == "/_ping" && (get || method == http.MethodHead):
		return OpPing, "", true
	case p == "/version" && get:
		return OpVersion, "", true
	case p == "/info" && get:
		return OpInfo, "", true
	case p == "/containers/json" && get:
		return OpList, "", true
	case p == "/events" && get:
		return OpEvents, "", true
	}
	c := containerRe.FindStringSubmatch(p)
	if c == nil {
		return 0, "", false
	}
	switch {
	case c[2] == "json" && get:
		return OpInspect, c[1], true
	case c[2] == "start" && method == http.MethodPost:
		return OpStart, c[1], true
	case c[2] == "stop" && method == http.MethodPost:
		return OpStop, c[1], true
	}
	return 0, "", false
}

// StripEnv removes Config.Env from a container inspect (it carries RCON_PASSWORD, CF_API_KEY…).
func StripEnv(inspect []byte) ([]byte, error) {
	var m map[string]json.RawMessage
	if err := json.Unmarshal(inspect, &m); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unreadable inspect: %w", err)
	}
	raw, ok := m["Config"]
	if !ok {
		return inspect, nil
	}
	var cfg map[string]json.RawMessage
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &cfg); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unreadable inspect: %w", err)
	}
	delete(cfg, "Env")
	clean, err := json.Marshal(cfg)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	m["Config"] = clean
	return json.Marshal(m)
}

// Policy decides on OpStart and OpStop: nil = allowed; an error is answered with 409 and its text. container is the id
// or the name as mc-router asked for it.
type Policy func(ctx context.Context, op Op, container string) error

// Options configures the proxy.
type Options struct {
	Listen   string // socket that is mounted in the router (host path: mirror mount of DATA_ROOT)
	Upstream string // real Docker socket
	Policy   Policy
	UID, GID int // socket owner (the mc-router user); UID < 0 = not changed
	Log      *slog.Logger
}

// Proxy is the HTTP server of the filtered socket.
type Proxy struct {
	opts Options
	rp   *httputil.ReverseProxy

	mu    sync.Mutex
	ln    net.Listener
	since time.Time
	err   error
}

// New prepares the proxy (it does not listen until Listen).
func New(o Options) *Proxy {
	if o.Log == nil {
		o.Log = slog.Default()
	}
	p := &Proxy{opts: o}
	p.rp = &httputil.ReverseProxy{
		Rewrite: func(r *httputil.ProxyRequest) {
			r.Out.URL.Scheme, r.Out.URL.Host, r.Out.Host = "http", "docker", "docker"
		},
		Transport: &http.Transport{
			DialContext: func(ctx context.Context, _, _ string) (net.Conn, error) {
				var d net.Dialer
				return d.DialContext(ctx, "unix", o.Upstream)
			},
			MaxIdleConns: 4, IdleConnTimeout: 30 * time.Second,
		},
		FlushInterval:  -1, // events arrive as soon as Docker emits them
		ModifyResponse: p.modify,
		ErrorHandler: func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, err error) {
			p.opts.Log.Warn("router proxy: Docker did not respond", "method", r.Method, "path", r.URL.Path, "err", err)
			writeErr(w, http.StatusBadGateway, "mcsm: Docker is not responding")
		},
	}
	return p
}

func (p *Proxy) modify(res *http.Response) error {
	op, _, _ := Classify(res.Request.Method, res.Request.URL.Path, res.Request.URL.RawPath)
	if op != OpInspect || res.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		return nil
	}
	body, err := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(res.Body, maxInspectBytes+1))
	res.Body.Close()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if len(body) > maxInspectBytes {
		return errors.New("inspect too large")
	}
	clean, err := StripEnv(body)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	res.Body = io.NopCloser(bytes.NewReader(clean))
	res.ContentLength = int64(len(clean))
	res.Header.Set("Content-Length", strconv.Itoa(len(clean)))
	return nil
}

// ServeHTTP applies the allowlist and the policy, and forwards what is allowed to the real Docker.
func (p *Proxy) ServeHTTP(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	op, container, ok := Classify(r.Method, r.URL.Path, r.URL.RawPath)
	if !ok {
		p.opts.Log.Warn("router proxy: request denied", "method", r.Method, "path", r.URL.Path)
		writeErr(w, http.StatusForbidden, "mcsm: operation not allowed for the router")
		return
	}
	if (op == OpStart || op == OpStop) && p.opts.Policy != nil {
		if err := p.opts.Policy(r.Context(), op, container); err != nil {
			p.opts.Log.Info("router proxy: start or stop denied", "container", container, "err", err)
			writeErr(w, http.StatusConflict, "mcsm: "+err.Error())
			return
		}
	}
	p.rp.ServeHTTP(w, r)
}

func writeErr(w http.ResponseWriter, code int, msg string) {
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	w.WriteHeader(code)
	_ = json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]string{"message": msg}) // error format of the Docker API
}

// Listen creates the socket (removing a previous one) and leaves it only for the mc-router user.
func (p *Proxy) Listen() error {
	ln, err := p.listen()
	p.mu.Lock()
	defer p.mu.Unlock()
	p.err = err
	if err == nil {
		p.ln, p.since = ln, time.Now()
	}
	return err
}

func (p *Proxy) listen() (net.Listener, error) {
	path := p.opts.Listen
	if len(path) > MaxSocketPath {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("the router socket path is too long (%d > %d bytes): use a shorter DATA_ROOT", len(path), MaxSocketPath)
	}
	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(path), 0o755); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if err := os.Remove(path); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist) {
		return nil, err
	}
	ln, err := net.Listen("unix", path)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if err := secure(path, p.opts.UID, p.opts.GID); err != nil {
		ln.Close()
		return nil, err
	}
	return ln, nil
}

// secure leaves the socket at 0600 for uid. Without permission to change the owner (tests without root) it opens it to
// everyone (0666): this only happens on development machines; the agent runs as root.
func secure(path string, uid, gid int) error {
	if uid >= 0 {
		if err := os.Chown(path, uid, gid); err != nil {
			if os.Geteuid() == 0 {
				return err
			}
			return os.Chmod(path, 0o666)
		}
	}
	return os.Chmod(path, 0o600)
}

// Serve serves the socket until ctx ends; afterwards it removes it. Without a previous Listen it does nothing.
func (p *Proxy) Serve(ctx context.Context) {
	p.mu.Lock()
	ln := p.ln
	p.mu.Unlock()
	if ln == nil {
		return
	}
	srv := &http.Server{Handler: p, ReadHeaderTimeout: 10 * time.Second}
	go func() {
		<-ctx.Done()
		_ = srv.Close() // also cuts the open event streams
	}()
	if err := srv.Serve(ln); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, http.ErrServerClosed) {
		p.opts.Log.Error("router proxy: the socket stopped serving", "err", err)
	}
	_ = os.Remove(p.opts.Listen)
}

// Ready is nil if the socket is listening; otherwise, why not.
func (p *Proxy) Ready() error {
	p.mu.Lock()
	defer p.mu.Unlock()
	if p.ln != nil {
		return nil
	}
	if p.err != nil {
		return p.err
	}
	return errors.New("the Docker socket proxy is not listening yet")
}

// Since is when the current socket started listening (zero if it is not listening).
func (p *Proxy) Since() time.Time {
	p.mu.Lock()
	defer p.mu.Unlock()
	return p.since
}
```

```go
func TestReconcileWaitsForTheProxy(t *testing.T) {
	st, err := store.Open(filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "a.db"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	defer st.Close()

	_ = m.Apply(&mcsmv1.RouterSpec{HostPort: 25565})
	obs := m.Reconcile(context.Background())
	if !strings.Contains(obs.Error, "proxy") || routerContainer(t, fake) != nil {

func TestRouterRestartsOnceAfterANewProxy(t *testing.T) {
	m, fake, _ := newManager(t)
	var since time.Time
	m.opts.ProxySince = func() time.Time { return since }
	ctx := context.Background()
	_ = m.Apply(&mcsmv1.RouterSpec{HostPort: 25565})
	if obs := m.Reconcile(ctx); obs.State != "running" {
		t.Fatalf("observed = %+v", obs)
	}
	stops := calls(fake, "Stop ")
	since = time.Now() // the agent restarted: new proxy, router from before
	if obs := m.Reconcile(ctx); obs.State != "running" || obs.Error != "" {
		t.Fatalf("observed = %+v", obs)
	}
	if calls(fake, "Stop ") != stops+1 {
		t.Fatal("the router from before the proxy is restarted")
	}
	if m.Reconcile(ctx); calls(fake, "Stop ") != stops+1 {
		t.Fatal("only once")
	}
}
```

```go
type Options struct {
	// Name of the container: ContainerName if empty (the tests with real Docker use mcsm-test-…).
	Name    string
	Network string
	// ProxySocket is the HOST path of the agent's Docker proxy socket (dockerproxy), which is mounted in the router at
	// RouterSocket. ProxyReady (optional) says whether it listens; ProxySince (optional), since when: a router started
	// before the current proxy (the agent restarted) is restarted once.
	ProxySocket string
	ProxyReady  func() error
	ProxySince  func() time.Time
	Now         func() time.Time
	// FilesDir is a HOST directory (the agent sees it at the same path: mirror mount of DATA_ROOT) where the autoscale
	// player list that is mounted in the router is written.
	FilesDir string
}
```
