In `diffSpecs`, the `mods` and `plugins` sections also have their own line («Mods (section)» / «Plugins (section)»: «(not managed)» if missing, «managed by the panel» if present). /**
```ts
 * L5 of Plan 3: without online mode the server does not check accounts, so anyone can join with the name of an
 * operator or of someone on the whitelist.
 */
export function offlineModeWarning(spec: { properties?: Record<string, string>; ops?: string[]; whitelist?: { players: string[] } }): string | null {
  if (spec.properties?.["online-mode"] !== "false") return null;
  if (!(spec.ops?.length || spec.whitelist?.players.length)) return null;
  return "Online mode is off: anyone can join with the name of an operator or of someone on the whitelist. Turn it on (Properties → online-mode) if the server is reachable from outside home.";
}
```

```ts
import { defineConfig } from "@playwright/test";

/** Minimal E2E (Plan 9, spec §11): one browser, serial, against the compose brought up by deploy/e2e/run.sh. */
export default defineConfig({
  testDir: "./tests",
  fullyParallel: false,
  workers: 1,
  retries: 0,
  timeout: 12 * 60_000,
  expect: { timeout: 30_000 },
  reporter: [["list"]],
  outputDir: "./test-results",
  use: {
    baseURL: process.env.E2E_BASE_URL ?? "http://panel:8080",
    headless: true,
    viewport: { width: 1440, height: 900 },
    locale: "es-ES",
    colorScheme: "dark",
    screenshot: "only-on-failure",
    trace: "retain-on-failure",
  },
  projects: [
    { name: "setup", testMatch: /auth\.setup\.ts/ },
    { name: "e2e", testMatch: /\.spec\.ts/, dependencies: ["setup"], use: { storageState: ".auth/admin.json" } },
  ],
});
```

`panel/apps/e2e/tests/helpers.ts`:
```ts
import { expect, type APIRequestContext, type Page } from "@playwright/test";

export const SHOTS = process.env.E2E_SHOTS ?? "screenshots";
export const NODE_A = process.env.E2E_NODE_A ?? "e2e-a";
export const NODE_B = process.env.E2E_NODE_B ?? "e2e-b";
/** Slug test-e2e-uno → container mcsm-test-e2e-uno (test prefix). */
export const SERVER = "test-e2e-uno";

/** Full-page capture for the visual review (the controller looks at them). */
export async function shot(page: Page, name: string): Promise<void> {
  await page.screenshot({ path: `${SHOTS}/${name}.png`, fullPage: true });
}

export async function nodeId(api: APIRequestContext, name: string): Promise<string> {
  const nodes = (await (await api.get("/api/nodes")).json()) as { id: string; name: string }[];
  const n = nodes.find((x) => x.name === name);
  expect(n, `node ${name}`).toBeTruthy();
  return n!.id;
}

export async function serverByName(api: APIRequestContext, name: string): Promise<{ id: string; nodeId: string } & Record<string, unknown>> {
  const list = (await (await api.get("/api/servers")).json()) as ({ id: string; name: string; nodeId: string } & Record<string, unknown>)[];
  const s = list.find((x) => x.name === name);
  expect(s, `server ${name}`).toBeTruthy();
  return s!;
}

/** Polls a real system (Docker, itzg) until fn returns something. In the E2E, waiting is expected. */
export async function poll<T>(fn: () => Promise<T | null | undefined | false>, what: string, timeoutMs: number, everyMs = 2_000): Promise<T> {
  const deadline = Date.now() + timeoutMs;
  for (;;) {
    const v = await fn();
    if (v) return v;
    if (Date.now() > deadline) throw new Error(`timed out waiting for ${what}`);
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, everyMs));
  }
}

/** CSP errors in the browser console (L10: the policy must not break the web). */
export function watchCsp(page: Page): string[] {
  const errors: string[] = [];
  page.on("console", (m) => { if (m.type() === "error" && /Content Security Policy/i.test(m.text())) errors.push(m.text()); });
  return errors;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Override of the deployment compose**

`deploy/e2e/compose.e2e.yml`:
```yaml
# Override of deploy/docker-compose.yml for the UI E2E (deploy/e2e/run.sh, project mcsm-test-e2e).
# Everything with the mcsm-test-e2e prefix: images, server network, router and ports that do not clash with production
# (8080 and 25565–25600). DATA_ROOT and ENROLL_TOKEN are set by run.sh.
services:
  panel:
    image: mcsm-test-e2e-panel:latest
    ports: !override ["127.0.0.1:${E2E_PANEL_PORT:-18080}:8080"]
    environment:
      PANEL_PUBLIC_URL: ""
      PANEL_SETUP_TOKEN: ${E2E_SETUP_TOKEN:?run.sh define E2E_SETUP_TOKEN}
  agent:
    image: mcsm-test-e2e-agent:latest
    environment:
      MCSM_NETWORK: mcsm-test-e2e-net
      MCSM_ROUTER_NAME: mcsm-test-e2e-router
networks:
  mcsm:
    name: mcsm-test-e2e-net
    external: true
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `run.sh`**

`deploy/e2e/run.sh`:
```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# UI E2E (Plan 9, spec §11): the deployment compose (project mcsm-test-e2e), a second node on docker:dind and
# headless Playwright in a container. It only creates and deletes mcsm-test-e2e* resources.
#   deploy/e2e/run.sh up     builds and starts everything (panel configured, nodes e2e-a and e2e-b connected)
#   deploy/e2e/run.sh test   runs Playwright against what is up (screenshots in panel/apps/e2e/screenshots/)
#   deploy/e2e/run.sh down   removes everything (including the servers the agents created)
#   deploy/e2e/run.sh [all]  up + test + down (down also if something fails; E2E_KEEP=1 leaves it up)
set -euo pipefail

ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/../.." && pwd)"
P=mcsm-test-e2e
NET="$P-net"                 # network of node A's servers (MCSM_NETWORK); this script creates it
DEFNET="${P}_default"        # compose network: panel, agent A, node B and Playwright
COMPOSE=(docker compose -p "$P" -f "$ROOT/deploy/docker-compose.yml" -f "$ROOT/deploy/e2e/compose.e2e.yml")
DATA="$ROOT/deploy/data/e2e" # never under /tmp: dind mounts its tmpfs there and would hide the bind
SHOTS="$ROOT/panel/apps/e2e/screenshots"
LOGS="$DATA/logs"
PORT="${E2E_PANEL_PORT:-18080}"
API="http://127.0.0.1:$PORT"
NODEB="$P-nodeb"
COOKIES="$DATA/cookies.txt"
DIND_IMAGE="docker:29-dind@sha256:0b6d18a4a222e71c88be6034926b4f6cde2fc33c2218643d03ccfddc65995470"
PW_IMAGE="mcr.microsoft.com/playwright:v1.64.0-noble@sha256:06a9939e57531807f8d5fd76ce44b53165ffb7d7501d87ab10e285c20b1e971f"
ALPINE="alpine:3.22@sha256:5291449c3df73caf6ed85e649dec1b9e818b39a5d8c871e97afc13e9cd5e8fa8"
MC_IMAGE="itzg/minecraft-server:java21"
export E2E_PANEL_PORT="$PORT"
export E2E_PASSWORD="${E2E_PASSWORD:-e2e-contrasena-segura}"
export E2E_SETUP_TOKEN="${E2E_SETUP_TOKEN:-e2e-setup-$(date +%s)}"

log() { printf '\n== %s\n' "$*"; }
json() { node -e 'let s="";process.stdin.on("data",d=>s+=d).on("end",()=>{const v=JSON.parse(s);console.log(eval(process.argv[1]))})' "$1"; }
mine() { docker ps -a --format '{{.Names}}' | grep '^mcsm-test-e2e' || true; }

up() {
  if [ -n "$(mine)" ]; then echo "There are already mcsm-test-e2e* containers: run '$0 down' first" >&2; exit 1; fi
  if ss -ltn "( sport = :$PORT )" | grep -q LISTEN; then echo "Port $PORT is busy" >&2; exit 1; fi
  mkdir -p "$DATA/a" "$DATA/b" "$LOGS" "$SHOTS"
  rm -f "$SHOTS"/*.png
  docker network inspect "$NET" >/dev/null 2>&1 || docker network create "$NET" >/dev/null

  log "images (deployment compose)"
  DATA_ROOT="$DATA/a" "${COMPOSE[@]}" build panel agent

  log "panel"
  DATA_ROOT="$DATA/a" "${COMPOSE[@]}" up -d panel
  for _ in $(seq 90); do curl -fsS "$API/api/health" >/dev/null 2>&1 && break; sleep 1; done
  curl -fsS "$API/api/health" >/dev/null
  curl -fsS -c "$COOKIES" -H 'content-type: application/json' \
    -d "{\"password\":\"$E2E_PASSWORD\",\"setupToken\":\"$E2E_SETUP_TOKEN\"}" "$API/api/auth/setup" >/dev/null
  # Settings: E2E port range, away from production's 25565–25600.
  curl -fsS -b "$COOKIES" -X PUT -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"gamePortRange":{"from":26600,"to":26620}}' "$API/api/settings" >/dev/null

  token() {
    curl -fsS -b "$COOKIES" -H 'content-type: application/json' -d "{\"nameHint\":\"$1\",\"dataRoot\":\"$2\"}" \
      "$API/api/nodes/enrollment-tokens" | json 'v.token'
  }

  log "node A: the compose agent, on the host's Docker"
  ENROLL_TOKEN="$(token e2e-a "$DATA/a")" DATA_ROOT="$DATA/a" "${COMPOSE[@]}" up -d agent

  log "node B: docker:dind with the agent inside"
  docker run -d --privileged --name "$NODEB" --hostname e2e-nodeb --network "$DEFNET" \
    -e DOCKER_TLS_CERTDIR= -v "$DATA/b:$DATA/b" "$DIND_IMAGE" >/dev/null
  for _ in $(seq 60); do docker exec "$NODEB" docker info >/dev/null 2>&1 && break; sleep 1; done
  docker image inspect "$MC_IMAGE" >/dev/null 2>&1 || docker pull "$MC_IMAGE"
  docker save "$P-agent:latest" | docker exec -i "$NODEB" docker load >/dev/null
  docker save "$MC_IMAGE" | docker exec -i "$NODEB" docker load >/dev/null
  local panel_ip
  panel_ip="$(docker inspect -f "{{(index .NetworkSettings.Networks \"$DEFNET\").IPAddress}}" "$P-panel-1")"
  # Inside dind the compose network's DNS does not reach: agent B goes to the panel by IP.
  docker exec "$NODEB" docker run -d --name "$P-agent-b" --restart unless-stopped --stop-timeout 30 \
    -v /var/run/docker.sock:/var/run/docker.sock -v "$DATA/b:$DATA/b" -v agent-b-data:/var/lib/mcsm-agent \
    -e PANEL_URL="http://$panel_ip:8080" -e ENROLL_TOKEN="$(token e2e-b "$DATA/b")" -e DATA_ROOT="$DATA/b" \
    -e MCSM_NETWORK="$NET" -e MCSM_ROUTER_NAME="$P-router-b" "$P-agent:latest" >/dev/null

  log "waiting for both nodes"
  local n=0
  for _ in $(seq 90); do
    n="$(curl -fsS -b "$COOKIES" "$API/api/nodes" | json 'v.filter(x=>x.status==="online").length')"
    [ "$n" = 2 ] && break
    sleep 2
  done
  [ "$n" = 2 ] || { echo "the nodes did not connect (see $LOGS after '$0 down')" >&2; exit 1; }
}

run_tests() {
  log "Playwright (headless, official image with the browsers)"
  docker run --rm --name "$P-playwright" --network "$DEFNET" --ipc=host --user "$(id -u):$(id -g)" \
    -e HOME=/tmp -e CI=1 -e E2E_BASE_URL=http://panel:8080 -e E2E_PASSWORD -e E2E_NODE_A=e2e-a -e E2E_NODE_B=e2e-b \
    -e E2E_SHOTS="$SHOTS" -v "$ROOT:$ROOT" -w "$ROOT/panel/apps/e2e" "$PW_IMAGE" ./node_modules/.bin/playwright test
}

down() {
  log "cleanup (only mcsm-test-e2e*)"
  mkdir -p "$LOGS"
  docker logs "$P-panel-1" >"$LOGS/panel.log" 2>&1 || true
  docker logs "$P-agent-1" >"$LOGS/agent-a.log" 2>&1 || true
  docker exec "$NODEB" docker logs "$P-agent-b" >"$LOGS/agent-b.log" 2>&1 || true
  docker rm -f "$P-playwright" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  docker rm -f -v "$NODEB" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true # with its /var/lib/docker (anonymous volume)
  DATA_ROOT="$DATA/a" ENROLL_TOKEN= "${COMPOSE[@]}" down -v --remove-orphans >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  local left
  left="$(mine)" # servers and router that agent A created in the host's Docker
  if [ -n "$left" ]; then docker rm -f $left >/dev/null; fi
  docker network rm "$NET" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  docker image rm "$P-panel:latest" "$P-agent:latest" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  # Worlds and backups belong to root (itzg, agent): they are removed from a container.
  docker run --rm -v "$DATA:/d" "$ALPINE" sh -c 'rm -rf /d/a /d/b /d/cookies.txt' >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
}

case "${1:-all}" in
  up) up ;;
  test) run_tests ;;
  down) down ;;
  all)
    start=$(date +%s)
    trap 'rc=$?; [ "${E2E_KEEP:-0}" = 1 ] || down; echo "E2E: $(( $(date +%s) - start )) s (exit $rc). Screenshots: $SHOTS"; exit $rc' EXIT
    up
    run_tests
    ;;
  *) echo "usage: $0 [all|up|test|down]" >&2; exit 2 ;;
esac
```
`chmod +x deploy/e2e/run.sh`.

`panel/apps/e2e/tests/auth.setup.ts`:
```ts
import { expect, test as setup } from "@playwright/test";
import { shot } from "./helpers";

setup("sign in with the administrator password", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto("/login");
  await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "Sign in" })).toBeVisible();
  await shot(page, "01-login");
  await page.getByLabel("Password", { exact: true }).fill(process.env.E2E_PASSWORD!);
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Sign in" }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/$/);
  await expect(page.getByRole("link", { name: "New server" })).toBeVisible();
  await page.context().storageState({ path: ".auth/admin.json" });
});
```

```ts
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";
import { NODE_A, SERVER, poll, shot, watchCsp } from "./helpers";

test("create a server, see it start, use the console and see its charts", async ({ page, request }) => {
  const csp = watchCsp(page);
  await page.goto("/servers/new");
  await page.getByRole("radio", { name: /From scratch/ }).click();
  await page.locator("#sf-type").click();
  await page.getByRole("option", { name: "Vanilla", exact: true }).click();
  await page.locator("#sf-version").fill("1.21.4");
  await page.locator("#sf-java").click();
  await page.getByRole("option", { name: "java21", exact: true }).click();
  await page.locator("#sf-memory").fill("1G");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: /Next/ }).click();
  await page.locator("#ns-node").click();
  await page.getByRole("option", { name: NODE_A }).click();
  await page.locator("#ns-name").fill(SERVER);
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: /Next/ }).click();
  await shot(page, "02-wizard-review");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Create server" }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/servers\/[0-9a-f-]+$/);
  const id = page.url().split("/").pop()!;
  const created = await (await request.get(`/api/servers/${id}`)).json();
  expect(created.spec.ports.game).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(26600); // range from Settings
  expect(created.spec.ports.game).toBeLessThanOrEqual(26620);

  // Real itzg start: downloads the 1.21.4 jar and generates the world.
  await poll(async () => (await (await request.get(`/api/servers/${id}`)).json()).observed?.mc?.online, "the server running", 6 * 60_000);

  // Overview charts: one sample every 10 s while it is running.
  await expect(page.getByText(/No data in this range/)).toBeHidden({ timeout: 90_000 });

  await page.getByRole("tab", { name: "Console" }).click();

  await expect(log).toContainText("Done (", { timeout: 60_000 });
  await page.getByLabel("Command", { exact: true }).fill("list");
  await page.keyboard.press("Enter");
  await expect(log).toContainText(/There are 0 of a max of \d+ players online/);
  await shot(page, "04-console");

  await page.getByRole("tab", { name: "Configuration" }).click();
  await expect(page.getByTestId("config-form")).toBeVisible();
  await shot(page, "05-configuration");

});
```

`panel/apps/e2e/tests/20-backup.spec.ts`:
```ts
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";
import { SERVER, poll, serverByName, shot, watchCsp } from "./helpers";

type Job = { type: string; status: string; message: string };

test("manual backup and restore: what was written after the backup disappears", async ({ page, request }) => {
  const csp = watchCsp(page);
  const s = await serverByName(request, SERVER);
  await page.goto(`/servers/${s.id}`);
  await page.getByRole("tab", { name: "Backups" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: /Create backup now/ }).click();
  const backup = await poll(async () => {
    const list = (await (await request.get(`/api/servers/${s.id}/backups`)).json()) as { id: string; filename: string; trigger: string; status: string }[];
    return list.find((b) => b.trigger === "manual" && b.status === "ok");
  }, "the manual backup", 3 * 60_000);
  await expect(page.getByTestId(`backup-row-${backup.filename}`)).toBeVisible();
  await shot(page, "06-backups");

  // A file written after the backup: after restoring it must not be there.
  const write = await request.post(`/api/servers/${s.id}/files/content`, { data: { path: "/e2e-marca.txt", content: "written after the backup" } });
  expect(write.status()).toBe(204);

  await page.getByTestId(`backup-row-${backup.filename}`).getByRole("button", { name: "Restore" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("alertdialog").getByRole("button", { name: "Restore" }).click();
  await poll(async () => {
    const jobs = (await (await request.get(`/api/jobs?serverId=${s.id}`)).json()) as Job[];
    const r = jobs.find((j) => j.type === "restore");
    if (r?.status === "failed") throw new Error(`restore failed: ${r.message}`);
    return r?.status === "ok";
  }, "the restore", 5 * 60_000);
  expect((await request.get(`/api/servers/${s.id}/files/content?path=${encodeURIComponent("/e2e-marca.txt")}`)).status()).toBe(404);
  await poll(async () => (await (await request.get(`/api/servers/${s.id}`)).json()).observed?.mc?.online, "the server running again", 5 * 60_000);

  await page.getByRole("tab", { name: "Files" }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText("server.properties")).toBeVisible();
  await shot(page, "07-files");
  await page.getByRole("tab", { name: "History" }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("region", { name: "Server jobs" })).toBeVisible();
  await shot(page, "08-history");
  expect(csp).toEqual([]);
});
```

`panel/apps/e2e/tests/30-migrate.spec.ts`:
```ts
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";
import { NODE_B, SERVER, nodeId, poll, serverByName, shot, watchCsp } from "./helpers";

test("migrate the server to the second node (dind) and see it running there", async ({ page, request }) => {
  const csp = watchCsp(page);
  const s = await serverByName(request, SERVER);
  const target = await nodeId(request, NODE_B);
  await page.goto(`/servers/${s.id}`);
  await page.getByRole("tab", { name: "Migrate" }).click();
  await page.locator("#mig-target").click();
  await page.getByRole("option", { name: NODE_B }).click();
  await expect(page.locator("#mig-port")).toHaveAttribute("placeholder", /^\d+$/);
  await shot(page, "09-migrate");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: /Migrate…/ }).click();
  await page.getByRole("alertdialog").getByRole("button", { name: "Migrate" }).click();
  await poll(async () => {
    const m = (await (await request.get(`/api/servers/${s.id}/migration`)).json()).migration as { phase: string; error: string | null } | null;
    if (m?.phase === "failed") throw new Error(`migration failed: ${m.error}`);
    return m?.phase === "done";
  }, "the migration", 10 * 60_000, 3_000);
  await poll(async () => {
    const x = await (await request.get(`/api/servers/${s.id}`)).json();
    return x.nodeId === target && x.observed?.mc?.online;
  }, "the server running on node B", 5 * 60_000);
  await page.reload();
  await expect(page.getByText(NODE_B).first()).toBeVisible();
  await shot(page, "10-migrated");
  expect(csp).toEqual([]);
});
```

```ts
import { expect, test } from "@playwright/test";
import { NODE_A, nodeId, poll, shot, watchCsp } from "./helpers";

test("real token rotation and screenshots of the screens pending visual review", async ({ page, request }) => {
  const csp = watchCsp(page);
  const a = await nodeId(request, NODE_A);

  // Token rotation against the real agent: it reconnects with the new one and is no longer pending.
  const rot = await request.post(`/api/nodes/${a}/rotate-token`);
  expect(rot.status()).toBe(200);
  await poll(async () => {
    const n = ((await (await request.get("/api/nodes")).json()) as { id: string; status: string; tokenRotationPending: boolean }[]).find((x) => x.id === a);
    return n?.status === "online" && !n.tokenRotationPending;
  }, "node A with the new token", 60_000);

  await page.goto("/");
  await expect(page.getByRole("group", { name: "Node usage" }).first()).toBeVisible({ timeout: 60_000 });
  await shot(page, "11-panel-node-cards");

  await page.goto("/nodes");
  await expect(page.getByRole("link", { name: NODE_A })).toBeVisible();
  await shot(page, "12-nodes-router");

  await page.goto(`/nodes/${a}`);
  await expect(page.getByText(/No data in this range/)).toBeHidden({ timeout: 60_000 });
  await expect(page.locator("canvas").first()).toBeVisible();
  await shot(page, "13-node-detail");

  await page.goto(`/nodes/${a}/import`);
  await page.waitForLoadState("networkidle");
  await shot(page, "14-import");

  await page.goto("/settings");
  await expect(page.getByLabel("From")).toHaveValue("26600");
  await shot(page, "15-settings");

  await page.goto("/events");
  await expect(page.getByTestId("event-token_rotated").first()).toBeVisible();
  await shot(page, "16-events");

  await page.goto("/jobs");
  await shot(page, "17-jobs");
  expect(csp).toEqual([]);
});
```

### Task 35: Documentation

**Agent:** infra-dev

**Files:**

**Interfaces:** none in code.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the sections**

Changes to `README.md` (English, same tone as the rest):
1. **Getting started**, step 4: the freshly installed panel shows in its log an **initial setup token** (`docker compose -f deploy/docker-compose.yml logs panel`, line «Initial setup token»), which the web asks for to create the password; or set it yourself with `PANEL_SETUP_TOKEN` in `deploy/.env`. Explain that it closes the «first visitor» window (DNS rebinding).
2. New section **Settings** (after «Adding another machine»): password (closes the other sessions), panel URL (the saved one takes precedence over `PANEL_PUBLIC_URL`; it is the one in the enrollment command and the `Origin` accepted by the WebSocket), game port range (only for new servers without a port), CurseForge API key (warning if the saved one is not valid).
3. New section **Importing existing servers** (before «Migration between nodes»): Nodes → Import (or a node's detail); what it detects (`itzg/minecraft-server` containers not managed on that machine), review before adopting, adopt without stopping (it follows the original container until the first «Save and apply», which recreates it with `mcsm.*` labels and the same data folder), «Adopt and apply», data outside `DATA_ROOT` (`dataPath`: no backups or files; they are not migrated), Docker Compose containers (remove the service from the compose file after the first «Save and apply»), and how to **stop using an external folder**: delete the server without deleting data, move the folder to `DATA_ROOT/<slug>/data` and import it again (there is no button: the agent does not see folders outside `DATA_ROOT`).
4. New section **Events and crash loops** (after «Backups»): the Events page and the server's History; what is recorded (exit with error, out of memory, crash loop, container recreated, autosave disabled/re-enabled, token rotated); the loop: more than 3 exits with error in one hour → the agent stops it and does not start it again (neither Docker, nor the panel when reconnecting, nor mc-router) until you press **Start** or save a new configuration; the panel leaves it «stopped». 30-day / 10,000-event retention. If the panel was disconnected, the event may be lost, but the error is visible in the server header.
5. New section **Rotating a node's token** (within or after «Adding another machine»): Nodes → detail → Rotate token; the agent must be connected; the old token stays valid until the agent reconnects with the new one; if it does not confirm, wait 2 min before retrying; agents prior to Plan 9 do not support it (update them first).
6. **Security**: replace the bullet «The first visitor sets the password» with the one for the setup token; rewrite the router one: it **no longer** receives the Docker socket, but the agent's filtered socket (`DATA_ROOT/.mcsm-router/docker.sock`, only for its user), which only lets it list, inspect without environment variables, follow events and start/stop servers with non-reserved and non-latched hostnames; remove the sentence about the socket group and rootless Docker; add that when the agent is updated the router is recreated once and restarted every time the agent starts. Add a bullet about the headers (CSP, `frame-ancestors 'none'`) and the global login attempt limit. Mention that the console hides the RCON password and the CurseForge API key.
7. **Updating**: the agent shows its version (`agent/VERSION`) in Nodes; the base images are pinned by digest (how to update them: `docker buildx imagetools inspect`).
8. **Development**: «UI tests (E2E)»: `pnpm e2e` (= `bash deploy/e2e/run.sh`), what it brings up (project `mcsm-test-e2e`, port 18080, ports 26600–26620, node B on `docker:dind`, Playwright in a container), where the screenshots are (`panel/apps/e2e/screenshots/`) and the logs (`deploy/data/e2e/logs/`), `E2E_KEEP=1`, `run.sh up|test|down`, and that it only touches `mcsm-test-e2e*` resources.
9. In **Backups**: downloading a backup from the Backups tab (it goes through the panel in streaming; pre-restore data is not downloaded) and that a failed `save-on` is retried by itself every 15 s («Autosave disabled» event). Add a note about m8: if the agent falls right at the end of a restore, the job may be left «interrupted» with the data already restored; check the world before repeating it.

Notes for the controller at closing (candidates for the «Lessons learned» of `CLAUDE.md`): `Event` leaves through `App.events` (its own queue, not the session); the agent's engine is wrapped in `crashloop.Engine` (every own stop is noted) and the router proxy notes its own; the crash-loop retention is tied to the spec version; `app.settings.publicUrl()`/`gamePortRange()` replace `config.publicUrl`/`config.gamePortRange`; a node's pending token is valid until the first connection with it; `pnpm e2e` and its cleanup by prefix.

---

## Discarded

| Pending item | Reason |
|---|---|
| Plan 2 — §4 `nodes.settings` «stats interval» | The 10 s interval is fixed at the same time in the agent's sampling, the `metric_samples` slots and the aggregation (10 s / 1 min / 5 min); making it configurable touches the agent, the protocol and the queries without anyone needing it. |
| §4 `nodes.settings` «allowed port range» per node | The Ruling asks for the default range in Settings (global, Task 18); a per-node range adds nothing on a home LAN and would duplicate the port validation. |
| Plan 3 — L7 (`\` escapes in properties) | Not verified against itzg; validating it now in Go and zod would make the agent reject stored specs that start today. Low value against that risk. |
| Plan 3 — `CURSEFORGE_FILES`/`SPIGET_RESOURCES` not emitted when emptied | itzg's behaviour with an empty list is unverified (it requires a CurseForge key and real downloads), and emitting them changes the env and the hash of those servers (recreation). |
| Plan 3 — normalising `type` | It is only reached through the API (the web uses a selector); transforming it in zod would rewrite stored specs and version servers without real changes. |
| Plan 3 — splitting `SyncSpecs` / cap on servers per node (and m-o of wave A) | A typical spec takes 1–3 KiB: exceeding 4 MiB would need ~60 specs at the 64 KiB cap. Splitting opens races between `removed_ids` and the spec of the same slug in messages that the agent processes in parallel. |
| Plan 4 — m8 (job FAILED after the second rename with the restored data) | It is only the job's text: the data is fine. Touching recovery at startup (delicate code) for a message is not worth it; it is documented in the README (Task 35). |
| Plan 4 — m13 (`restartPolicy: always` + daemon restart creates an empty `data`) | The root fix (`Binds` → `Mounts`) changes the hash of every server (mass recreation on update) for a very unlikely case. |
| Plan 5 — N1 (the WS drop cancels a healthy upload) | Accepted by design in the Plan 5 ruling: the upload's Ack is tied to the session; decoupling it requires redoing the upload protocol. |
| Plan 6 — m9 (require the agent capability for `dataPath`/`adopt`) | Already covered: an agent prior to Plan 6 answers `unsupported` to `ImportAdopt` (409), and a server with `dataPath` is not migrated. Only a manual downgrade of an agent remains (the README asks to update the agents first). |
| Plan 6 — action «move data / remove `dataPath`» | The agent only sees `DATA_ROOT`: it cannot move an external folder, and removing `dataPath` without moving it would start an empty world. The manual procedure is documented (Task 35). |
| Plan 8 — m14 (auto scale-down of a server started by the agent with no connections) | Already resolved: the Plan 8 fix report checked it with a real mc-router (it puts to sleep a server that another one started). |
